Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and the multi-site ABIDE~I neuroimaging dataset show MS-WDRO consistently outperforms seven baselines in graph recovery, sample efficiency, and downstream diagnostic utility, with the largest gains in the sample-scarce regime.

</details>


### [106] [Auditing Recorded Predictive Lead Service-Line Classifications Against Physical Verification: A Statewide Study of New York](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19922)
*Muhammad Sarmad Sohail*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究审查了纽约州153个地区使用预测模型确定供水管线材质的情况，发现纽约市等7个地区的模型预测结果与实际情况存在矛盾。纽约市使用模型将43,215个地址标记为"已知非铅"，但基于年代和验证数据的估算显示其中可能存在1,150-1,450条铅管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国铅铜规则修订允许供水公司使用预测模型而非物理检查来确定供水管线材质。纽约州公开了各地区使用的材质确定方法，为验证预测模型的准确性提供了独特机会。研究者希望评估这些预测模型的可靠性，特别是关注那些完全依赖模型而没有进行物理验证的情况。

Method: 筛选纽约州153个至少对100个地址使用模型分类的地区。比较模型预测结果与物理验证结果，分析建筑年代分布差异。使用6种考虑年代因素的估算器来估计模型标记为非铅的地址中可能存在的铅管线数量。

Result: 75个地区（49%）模型预测结果零方差。其中7个地区的模型结果与自有验证数据矛盾，6个无法用采样偏差解释。纽约市案例最大：模型标记43,215个地址为"已知非铅"，但估算显示其中可能有1,150-1,450条铅管线。模型分类群体房屋更新（中位建成年份1984年vs 1930年），年代因素解释约1/3的差异。发现7,782个模型分类地址位于1940年前建筑中。

Conclusion: 依赖预测模型而不进行物理验证可能导致铅管线漏检。纽约市的案例尤其值得关注：模型预测与验证数据存在明显矛盾，大量较老建筑被模型标记为非铅，且发现数据复制问题。研究建议加强模型验证监管，确保预测模型得到适当验证。

Abstract: Under the US Lead and Copper Rule Revisions, a utility may determine a service line's material with a predictive model instead of inspecting it. New York State publishes, per address, which method was used. Almost no address carries both a model classification and a physical verification, so the check is between populations within a utility rather than paired addresses. We screen all 153 New York localities that classified at least 100 addresses this way. Seventy-five (49%), covering 125,990 addresses or 57% of those screened, record one value. Zero variance alone is not misconduct: 68 of the 75 match their own verification or have too little to test. Seven are contradicted by their own crews, six beyond any sampling explanation. Five are boroughs of New York City, which file as one system; one is East Rochester, 550 km away. New York City is the largest case: a predictive model is the recorded basis for 43,215 addresses, and on all of them the recorded material is "Known Other". The city records "Unknown" on 121,779 addresses, 1,880 already excavated, and lead on 120,692. In the model bucket both counts are zero, and the 95% upper bound on the rate is 0.0085%. Across the rest of New York the same method records lead or the hedge "Unknown but could be lead" on 12.21% of 176,888 addresses, a comparison whose weaknesses we report. The model-cleared population is newer, median year built 1984 against 1930, and construction era accounts for about a third of the gap and not the rest: holding era fixed, records-based classification finds lead at 4.3-31.9%, physical verification at 1.5-14.5%, the model in no era. Six era-aware estimators place the expected lead lines among them at 1,150-1,450. Two findings need no comparison: 7,782 of these addresses are in pre-1940 buildings, and the archived 2025 snapshot shows the public-side determination was copied from a customer-side model output.

</details>


### [107] [G-MARK: Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Cooperative Driving via Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19964)
*Bhavya Gupta,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出G-MARK框架，通过构建溯源感知知识图谱显式保留多智能体观测信息，在自动驾驶协同场景下显著提升遮挡推理精度和决策性能，同时大幅降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统面临部分可观测性问题，安全关键物体可能被遮挡或仅对邻近联网车辆可见。现有协同驾驶方法将多智能体证据压缩为潜在特征或隐藏多模态状态，导致丢失观测来源归属、自车可见性、冲突证据处理等关键信息，影响下游决策质量。

Method: 提出G-MARK（Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning with Knowledge graphs）框架：1）将协同以对象为中心的观测转换为显式的溯源感知知识图谱（KGs），保留对象假设及其来源归属、自车与协作车辆可见性、不确定性、冲突、空间关系和规划相关上下文；2）从知识图谱推导共享特征表示，支持轻量级任务头进行对象推理、运动预测、控制选择和轨迹预测。

Result: 相比最先进基线方法：遮挡推理精度提升42.2%；控制选择错误率降低13.1%；在结构化通信负载减少25.6倍的情况下实现相当的轨迹规划精度。

Conclusion: G-MARK通过显式构建溯源感知知识图谱，成功解决了现有协同驾驶方法中信息模糊化问题，在保持规划性能的同时显著提升了推理准确性和通信效率，为部分可观测环境下的多智能体协同驾驶提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems must operate under partial observability, where safety-critical objects may be occluded or visible only to neighboring connected vehicles. Vehicle-to-vehicle cooperation can reduce this uncertainty, but existing cooperative driving methods often compress multi-agent evidence into latent features or hidden multimodal states. As a result, they obscure which agent observed each object, whether the object is visible to the ego vehicle, and how conflicting evidence affects downstream decisions. We propose G-MARK, a grounded multi-agent reasoning framework that converts cooperative object-centric observations into explicit provenance-aware knowledge graphs (KGs). The resulting KGs preserve object hypotheses together with their source attribution, ego-versus-partner visibility, uncertainty, conflicts, spatial relations, and planning-relevant context. G-MARK then derives a shared feature representation from these KGs, enabling lightweight task heads to support object reasoning, motion prediction, control selection, and trajectory forecasting. Compared with the state-of-the-art baseline, GMARK improves occlusion reasoning accuracy by 42.2%, reduces control-selection error by 13.1%, and achieves comparable trajectory-planning accuracy with a 25.6x smaller structured communication payload. Our code is available at https://github.com/bhavyagupta98/g-mark.

</details>


### [108] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究分析了机器学习数据压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的盈亏平衡点，比较了ML训练和推理的基础设施碳排放与减少磁盘存储带来的碳减排效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习技术的广泛应用，其在数据处理中的环境可持续性问题日益受到关注。研究旨在评估ML压缩算法的环境影响，探索其是否能实现净环境效益。

Method: 以基于机器学习的无损压缩算法为例，估算ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳排放当量，同时计算因减少磁盘存储需求而产生的碳排放节省量，对比分析两者的盈亏平衡点。

Result: 研究提供了ML压缩算法碳排放与节省量的对比估算框架，确定了实现环境可持续性的盈亏平衡条件，为评估ML技术环境效益提供了量化依据。

Conclusion: 机器学习压缩算法的环境可持续性取决于达到盈亏平衡点，需要权衡训练推理的碳排放成本与存储优化的环境效益，为绿色ML技术应用提供决策参考。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

</details>


### [109] [Scale-Aware Pretraining of Time Series Foundation Models via Multi-Patch Token Alignment and Hybrid Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20005)
*Taihua Chen,Xiang Ma,Yixin Zhang,Tailin Zhan,Manyu Sun,Lizhen Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SATS模型，通过尺度感知的令牌对齐机制和混合掩码策略解决异构数据集上的采样频率差异问题，在LSTF基准上实现MSE提升9.2%和模型效率提高65.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨异构数据集预训练时间序列基础模型时，不同采样频率的处理是一个关键挑战。现有方法要么采用数据集特定的patch大小和分离的前馈网络（导致表示碎片化），要么强制使用固定patch大小（忽视了固有的时间变化），这两种方法都无法有效处理时间序列中的采样频率差异问题。

Method: 提出SATS（Scale-Aware Time Series）模型，包含两个核心组件：(1) 尺度感知令牌对齐机制，将patch大小视为显式的尺度概念，通过对比启发的对齐正则化项在不同尺度间对齐表示空间，同时保留独特的建模能力；(2) 混合掩码策略，结合随机掩码和连续掩码以捕获多尺度时间结构。

Result: 在LSTF基准测试上，SATS相比竞争基线实现MSE提升9.2%，在GIFT-Eval MASE上获得8.3%的提升。同时，SATS在实现最先进性能的同时，相比高级基线实现了65.6%的模型效率提升。

Conclusion: SATS有效解决了时间序列预训练中异构数据集的采样频率处理问题，证明了其在时间序列预训练中的有效性和可扩展性，为跨数据集的时间序列基础模型预训练提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Pretraining time series foundation models across heterogeneous datasets necessitates effective handling of varying sampling frequencies. Current methods either employ dataset-specific patch sizes and separate FFNs, leading to fragmented representations, or enforce a fixed patch size that neglects inherent temporal variations. To address this, we propose SATS, featuring a scale-aware token alignment mechanism that treats patch size as an explicit notion of scale. By incorporating a contrastive-inspired alignment regularizer, SATS aligns representation spaces across scales while preserving distinct modeling capacities. Furthermore, a hybrid masking strategy combining random and contiguous masking is introduced to capture multi-scale temporal structures. Experimental results on LSTF benchmarks demonstrate that SATS achieves a 9.2% improvement in MSE and an 8.3% gain in GIFT-Eval MASE compared to competitive baselines. Notably, SATS consistently delivers SOTA performance while achieving a 65.6% increase in model efficiency over advanced baselines, highlighting its effectiveness and scalability in time series pretraining.

</details>


### [110] [Systematic Evaluation of TabPFN-TS for Zero-Shot Probabilistic Heat Load Forecasting in District Heating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20024)
*Ben Spoek,Karim K. Ben Hicham,Kai Derzsi,Philipp Althaus,Alexander Mitsos,Dirk Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了TabPFN-TS在区域供热网络热负荷预测中的表现，发现其性能接近最先进的时间序列基础模型Chronos-2，同时具有更好的校准性能，并提出了一种多分辨率残差校正预测框架以提升长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域供热能源枢纽需要可靠的热负荷预测来实现高效运营调度。传统的预测工作流程在历史数据上训练特定系统模型，当网络通过新用户、改造或改变运行机制而发生变化时，这种方法会变得负担沉重。零样本时间序列基础模型和上下文预测提供了一个有前景的替代方案：它们可以在推理时从最近的观察中适应，而不是通过重复重新训练来适应。

Method: 该研究系统地将TabPFN-TS与时间序列基础模型和训练的机器学习基线进行概率热负荷预测的比较。与在大量真实时间序列集合上预训练的基础模型不同，TabPFN-TS依赖合成预训练数据。研究分析了协变量选择、上下文长度、时间分辨率和预测范围，在代表性运行周上进行分析，在全年范围内验证所选配置，并在第二个网络上测试可转移性。基于诊断发现，提出了多分辨率残差校正预测器，将低频基础预测器与短期残差预测器相结合。

Result: 研究结果确定了每小时24小时预测配合12周滚动上下文和环境温度是一个简约的高性能配置；较长的上下文窗口并不能提高准确性。TabPFN-TS在确定性精度上仍然接近Chronos-2，在主要数据集上达到CVRMSE值为13.06%对比12.48%，并且在日排名比较中处于临界差异阈值范围内。虽然Chronos-2实现了最低的全年总误差，但TabPFN-TS显示出更好的经验校准。

Conclusion: TabPFN-TS作为基于合成数据预训练的模型，在区域供热网络热负荷预测任务中表现优异，与基于真实数据预训练的最先进基础模型性能相当，且具有更好的校准性能。多分辨率残差校正预测框架的提出为改善长期规划精度提供了新思路。研究表明零样本预测方法在区域供热领域具有实用价值，能够避免传统方法在网络变化时需要重新训练的负担。

Abstract: District heating energy hubs require reliable heat load forecasts for efficient operational scheduling. Conventional forecasting workflows train system-specific models on historical data, which can become burdensome when networks change through new consumers, retrofits, or changing operating regimes. Zero-shot time-series foundation models and in-context forecasting offer a promising alternative: they can adapt at inference time from recent observations rather than by repeated retraining. This study systematically evaluates TabPFN-TS against time-series foundation models and trained machine-learning baselines for probabilistic heat load forecasting in district heating networks. Unlike foundation models pretrained on large collections of real time series, TabPFN-TS relies on synthetic pretraining data, which avoids direct pretraining-test overlap but raises the question of whether the learned prior captures district heating dynamics. We analyze covariate choice, context length, temporal resolution, and prediction horizon on representative operating weeks, validate the selected configuration over a full year, and test transferability on a second network. The results identify hourly 24-hour forecasting with a 12-week rolling context and ambient temperature as a parsimonious high-performing configuration; longer context windows do not improve accuracy. TabPFN-TS remains close to Chronos-2 in deterministic accuracy, reaching CVRMSE values of 13.06% versus 12.48% on the main dataset, and lies within the critical-difference threshold in the daily-rank comparison. Although Chronos-2 achieves the lowest aggregate full-year error, TabPFN-TS shows better empirical calibration. Finally, the diagnostic findings motivate a Multi-Resolution Residual-Correction Forecaster that combines a low-frequency Base Forecaster with a short-horizon Residual Forecaster to improve longer-horizon planning accuracy.

</details>


### [111] [CLaST: Context-aware Contrastive VAE for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20025)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Petr Sokerin,Vitaliy Pozdnyakov,Ilya Kuleshov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CLaST，一种基于VAE的概率多变量时间序列预测框架，通过对比损失函数学习保留观测间上下文相似性的嵌入表示，在九个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型在概率预测中难以捕捉内部时间依赖性，导致潜在表示表达能力有限，影响了预测性能。

Method: 提出CLaST框架，基于变分自编码器(VAE)，创新性地引入对比损失函数来学习能够保留观测间上下文相似性的嵌入表示。

Result: 在九个广泛使用的基准数据集上，CLaST在短期预测任务中CRPS提升最高达16.4%，NMAE提升最高达14.4%；在长期预测任务中CRPS和NMAE分别比次优方法提升最高达48.6%和25.1%。

Conclusion: CLaST通过保留上下文相似性的嵌入学习，有效解决了现有方法在捕捉时间依赖性方面的不足，在概率多变量时间序列预测任务中实现了显著且一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are widely used for time series forecasting in domains such as energy systems, finance, medicine, and transportation. In recent years, deep generative models have shown strong results on probabilistic forecasting, yet many conventional approaches struggle to capture internal temporal dependencies, leading to latent representations with limited expressive power. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{CLaST}, a VAE framework for probabilistic multivariate time series forecasting. Unlike existing generative models, CLaST learns embeddings that preserve contextual similarity between observations through our contrastive loss function. Experiments across nine widely adopted benchmarks demonstrate that CLaST consistently surpasses strong baseline methods. In short-term forecasting tasks, our approach achieves improvements of up to $16.4\%$ in CRPS and $14.4\%$ in NMAE over the second-best method. Furthermore, in long-term prediction CLaST attains superior overall performance, exceeding the second-best method by up to $48.6\%$ and $25.1\%$ in CRPS and NMAE, respectively.

</details>


### [112] [An Inclusive and Lightweight Approach to Federated Continual Learning for Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20038)
*Ioannis Theologitis,Debin Meng,Stylianos Eleftheriadis,Vasileios Lolis,Konstantinos Votis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCurv-DR方法，一种轻量级正则化联邦持续学习策略，在文化遗产数据场景下有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时平衡性能、公平性和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产数据分散在不同机构、受所有权限制无法共享、且持续演进，传统集中式学习方法无法适用，需要一种能够在不共享原始数据的情况下从分布式序列数据中学习的解决方案。

Method: 提出FedCurv-DR方法：基于正则化的联邦持续学习策略，通过累积跨客户端和跨经验的参数重要性估计来保护已学习知识，仅在固定间隔更新参数重要性，从而最小化通信和计算开销。在WikiArt图像数据集上使用流派分类任务进行评估，引入风格演化模拟持续学习场景。

Result: FedCurv-DR有效降低了灾难性遗忘程度，在性能、公平性和能耗效率之间实现了良好平衡，适用于文化遗产领域的可持续AI应用。

Conclusion: FedCurv-DR为文化遗产和数字人文领域提供了一种实用且可持续的联邦持续学习解决方案，能够在数据分布式和持续演进的约束下实现有效的知识学习与保护。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence can support cultural heritage and digital humanities through large-scale retrieval and analysis of digitized collections. However, cultural heritage data are often distributed across institutions, constrained by ownership and access restrictions, and continuously evolving over time. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) is well suited to this setting, as it enables models to learn from distributed and sequential data without sharing raw collections. In this paper, we propose FedCurv-DR, a lightweight, regularisation-based FCL strategy. The method accumulates parameter-importance estimates across clients and experiences to protect learned knowledge, while updating them only at fixed intervals to minimize communication and computation overhead. We evaluate FedCurv-DR in a continual learning scenario using the WikiArt image dataset for genre classification with evolving styles, reporting performance, energy, and fairness metrics. Our results show that FedCurv- DR reduces forgetting and balances performance, fairness, and energy efficiency for sustainable AI in cultural heritage.

</details>


### [113] [End-to-end Early Classification of Time Series in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20044)
*Aurélien Renault,Alexis Bondu,Antoine Cornuéjols,Vincent Lemaire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非平稳环境下的时间序列早期分类问题，提出了DQeND统一架构，通过端到端联合学习表示、分类和触发决策，在概念漂移场景中显著优于传统的分离式设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列早期分类方法大多假设平稳环境，并采用分离式设计（分类和触发模块独立优化），这种假设在概念漂移条件下严重限制了模型的适应能力。本文旨在挑战这一范式，研究非平稳条件下的ECTS问题。

Method: 本文基于强化学习提出了DQeND架构，该架构统一学习表示、分类和触发决策三个模块，实现了端到端的联合优化。同时，论文首次系统性地比较了分离式和端到端方法在受控漂移场景下的表现。

Result: 在多种漂移场景的广泛实验中，DQeND展现出强大的鲁棒性，在各种非平稳场景中持续优于分离式基线方法。消融研究进一步证实，联合更新表示和决策模块对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 端到端学习方法能够为动态环境中的时间序列早期分类提供更好的适应能力，研究结果激励进一步探索分离式设计的替代方案。

Abstract: Early Classification of Time Series (ECTS) requires making accurate decisions as early as possible in inherently online and evolving environments. Yet, most existing methods assume stationarity and rely on separable designs, where classification and triggering are optimized independently, an assumption that fundamentally limits their adaptability under drift. In this work, we challenge this paradigm and study ECTS under non-stationary conditions. We provide the first systematic comparison between separable and end-to-end approaches across controlled drifting scenarios. Building on Reinforcement Learning, we introduce DQeND, a unified architecture that jointly learns representation, classification, and triggering decisions, while remaining directly comparable to state-of-the-art separable baselines. Across a wide range of drifts, DQeND demonstrates strong robustness across various non-stationary scenarios, consistently outperforming separable baselines. An ablation study further highlights that jointly updating representation and decision modules is critical to these gains. Overall, our results indicate that end-to-end learning can offer improved adaptation capabilities for ECTS in dynamic environments, and motivate further investigation of alternatives to separable designs.

</details>


### [114] [DecoVAE: a Lightweight Interpretable Trend-Seasonal VAE Framework for Efficient Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20052)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DecoVAE，一种轻量级可解释的趋势-季节性VAE框架，通过领域特定归纳偏置显式分解时间序列，在预测精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率时间序列预测中，趋势和季节性动态建模需要专门方法，现有方法无法捕捉组件独特内在属性、缺乏可解释性或存在严重的内存和运行时开销问题。

Method: DecoVAE框架将时间序列显式分解为趋势和季节性组件：趋势流通过潜在轨迹上的微分正则化器（类似Hodrick-Prescott滤波器）强制结构平滑性；季节性流通过复数高斯VAE在频域操作，原生捕捉周期模式的振幅和相位。

Result: 在7个真实基准数据集上持续优于强基线：短期预测CRPS降低达14.96%、NMAE降低达23.30%；长期预测分别降低达52.68%和26.51%。同时模型权重减少达93%，速度提升达74%（相比次优方法）。

Conclusion: DecoVAE在显著提升预测精度的同时保持高效率，通过显式分解和领域特定归纳偏置实现了轻量级、可解释的概率时间序列预测。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting remains challenging, largely because modeling distinct trend and seasonal dynamics requires specialized approaches. Existing methods often fail to capture the unique inner properties of these components, lack interpretability, or suffer from heavy memory and runtime overhead. To address these limitations, we propose DecoVAE, a lightweight interpretable trend-seasonal VAE framework that explicitly decomposes time series into trend and seasonal components by applying domain-specific inductive biases. The trend stream enforces structural smoothness using a differential regularizer on the latent trajectory, analogous to the Hodrick-Prescott filter. Concurrently, the seasonal stream operates in the frequency domain via a complex Gaussian VAE, natively capturing the amplitude and phase of periodic patterns. Extensive evaluations across seven real-world benchmarks show that DecoVAE consistently outperforms strong baselines. It achieves reductions of up to 14.96\% in CRPS and 23.30\% in NMAE for short-term forecasting, and up to 52.68\% and 26.51\% for long-term horizons. Crucially, DecoVAE yields these accuracy gains while remaining highly efficient, reducing model weight by up to 93\% and accelerating speed by up to 74\% compared to the second-best method.

</details>


### [115] [Let's Scale Step by Step: Compute-Efficient Hyperparameter Transfer for Large-Scale Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20061)
*Nayeon Kim,Hojin Lee,Yunju Bak,Jaesun Park,Boseop Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的两步超参数迁移框架，通过在宽度缩放的模型间迁移学习率并外推至万亿token训练规模，以低成本确定大规模MoE模型的最优学习率，成功应用于155B参数基础模型的预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 架构虽然能在不显著增加计算成本的情况下扩展模型容量，但在极端规模（大模型尺寸和海量token预算）下，通过网格搜索优化超参数（尤其是学习率）在计算上不可行，亟需一种计算高效的超参数优化方法。

Method: 提出两步超参数迁移框架：1) 为使用多头潜在注意力(MLA)和Muon优化器的MoE架构制定了Maximal Update Parameterization (μP)适配方案，实现最优学习率在宽度缩放模型间的迁移；2) 建立预测性缩放定律，通过对小代理模型在有限预算下得到的最优值进行线性回归，将理想学习率外推至大规模训练周期（如10万亿tokens）。

Result: 在万亿token规模的学习率外推中实现了高保真度(R²=0.95)；成功将方法应用于从头预训练155B总参数、17B激活参数的基础模型，证明了小规模代理训练足以确定大规模MoE训练的最优学习率，大幅降低了消融实验成本。

Conclusion: 通过所提出的超参数迁移框架，可以在最小消融成本下准确预测全规模目标模型的最优配置，验证了代理模型训练在确定大规模MoE模型最优学习率方面的充分性，为大模型训练提供了计算高效的超参数优化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures significantly expand model capacity without a proportional increase in computational cost. However, optimizing their hyperparameters---particularly the learning rate---at extreme scales of both model size and token budget via sweeping remains computationally prohibitive. In this paper, we propose a compute-efficient, two-step hyperparameter transfer framework that estimates optimal learning rates for training large MoE models by transferring them across scaling model widths, and subsequently extrapolating to trillion-token horizons. First, we formulate a Maximal Update Parameterization ($μ$P) adaptation for MoE architectures utilizing Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and the Muon optimizer, demonstrating that optimal learning rates transfer consistently across width-scaled models. Second, we extend this transferability along the token dimension by establishing a predictive scaling law. By applying linear regression to the optimal values derived from small proxy models on limited budgets, we successfully extrapolate the ideal learning rate to massive training horizons (e.g., 10 trillion tokens) with high fidelity ($R^2=0.95$). Consequently, this indicates that proxy training on small models is sufficient to determine the optimal learning rate for the extensive training of large-scale MoEs. We apply the proposed methodology to pretrain our foundation model (155B total, 17B active parameters) from scratch, and the stable training and evaluation results validate that optimal configurations for full-scale target models can be accurately predicted with minimal ablation costs.

</details>


### [116] [Orthogonal JEPA: Factorized Predictive States for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20065)
*Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPAL框架，通过正交预测分解机制改进世界模型中的表示学习，解决了标准JEPA在复杂系统中对主导信号过度分配容量的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）使用单一目标嵌入和预测路径组织所有可预测内容，在复杂系统中会导致状态表示将冗余容量分配给主导信号，同时为次要预测结构提供微弱或冲突的梯度，限制了表示质量。

Method: 提出OPAL框架，采用正交预测分解方法：学习基矩阵将目标状态分解为多个正交分量；使用共享上下文表示，为每个分量分配专用预测分支；引入预测回归保持因子幅度、正交性目标避免重复方向、因子活动正则化和在线方差正则化防止编码器坍塌。预测分量最终合成为完整潜在状态。

Result: 在控制视觉、单细胞转录组学、纵向健康记录、连续控制和分子动力学等多个领域进行实验，验证了该方法的表示质量、预测能力、规划能力和长时程稳定性。

Conclusion: OPAL通过正交预测分解有效解决了世界模型中单一目标表示的局限性，能够更好地处理复杂系统中的多层次预测结构，在多种应用场景中展现了优越性能。

Abstract: World models construct latent states that support prediction, planning, and reasoning about an underlying system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a direct way to learn such states by predicting targets in representation space instead of reconstructing every detail of the observation. Standard JEPAs, however, organize all predictable content through one target embedding and one prediction pathway. In complex systems, this monolithic state can allocate redundant capacity to dominant signals while providing weak or conflicting gradients to less dominant predictive structure. We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [117] [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117)
*Hugo Porta,Emanuele Dalsasso,Chang Xu,Theo Gnassounou,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于地理位置调制的稀疏自编码器（SAE）和规则集成的SAE-Xplainers方法，用于解释深度学习模型在极端地球事件（火灾、热带气旋、大气河流）预测中的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天气和气候数据集为极端地球事件建模提供了新机遇，但深度学习模型缺乏可解释性限制了其在实际操作环境中的应用。虽然稀疏自编码器在文本和图像模态中已证明有效，但由于天气和气候数据的特殊性，将其应用于极端地球事件分析仍具挑战性。

Method: 引入两项创新：(1) 基于地理位置的SAE输入调制机制，用于捕捉环境模式的局部语义含义；(2) 集成规则的SAE-Xplainers方法，用于解释从复杂多模态环境预测因子中提取的高维特征。

Result: 在三种极端地球事件（火灾预测、热带气旋检测、大气河流检测）上验证了方法的有效性。SAE输入调制提升了重建性能和特征利用率；SAE-Xplainers能够将复杂的气候模式展开为与科学文献一致的人类可理解规则，同时支持特征吸收的识别。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了极端地球事件预测模型的可解释性问题，为天气和气候深度学习模型在实际操作环境中的部署提供了可信的解释框架，促进了对复杂气候模式的科学理解。

Abstract: The emergence of large-scale Weather and Climate (W&C) datasets offers new opportunities for modeling extreme Earth events (ExEE) and their impacts using deep learning. However, their adoption in operational settings remains limited by the lack of models' interpretability. While for conventional text and image modalities, tools such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have proven effective for extracting human-understandable concepts, their use for the analysis of ExEE remains challenging due to the nature of W&C data. To address this, we introduce (i) a geographic location-based modulation of the inputs of SAE to capture the local semantic meaning of environmental patterns, and (ii) an ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers to interpret the resulting high-dimensional features derived from complex, multi-modal environmental predictors. We evaluate our method on three ExEE types: the prediction of fires, and the detection of tropical cyclones and atmospheric rivers. We show that SAE input modulation improves both reconstruction performance and feature utilization, and that our SAE-Xplainers enable faithful interpretation of complex climatic patterns by unfolding them into human-understandable rules that are consistent with the scientific literature, while also supporting the identification of feature absorption.

</details>


### [118] [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149)
*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了神经网络山体阴影方法（Eduard系统）在密集城市环境中的表现，通过与传统的分析型山体阴影方法对比，发现尽管Eduard主要针对山区地形训练，但在参数调优后仍可在城市环境中产生具有 cartographic价值的可视化效果，为未来专门针对城市地形训练神经网络模型提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eduard是一个原本为模拟瑞士风格山体阴影而设计的机器学习系统，其训练数据主要来自山地景观。由于该系统并非为城市基础设施（建筑物、桥梁、街道、树木等）设计，研究旨在探索通过参数调整，这种基于自然地形训练的神经方法能否适应高度人工化的城市环境，并在某些情况下产生优于传统分析方法的结果。

Method: 使用卡尔加里市中心的高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，对比三种山体阴影方法：单向分析型山体阴影、多向分析型山体阴影，以及Eduard神经网络系统生成的山体阴影。实验重点调整地形类型、微观与宏观概括化、平坦区域细节等参数，同时保持神经网络的大尺度阴影风格不变。采用探索性比较分析框架，而非寻求通用最佳实践基准。

Result: 研究发现：1）在平坦区域细节表现方面，Eduard在参数调整后能够产生视觉上强烈且具有制图价值的结果；2）在地形类型和概括化参数方面，存在传统分析方法更可靠的场景；3）由于训练数据偏向阿尔卑斯地形，神经网络在城市环境存在特定的失败模式。研究识别了每种方法的适用场景和局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了针对自然地形训练的神经网络山体阴影方法可以通过参数调优适应城市环境，为制图实践提供有价值的替代方案。研究结论呼吁未来应针对城市地形专门进行神经网络模型的训练和评估工作，以克服当前模型训练偏差带来的局限性，推动地形表示技术的进一步发展。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [119] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Relation，一种优于传统注意力机制的token混合原语，在降低验证损失的同时实现了最高4.41倍的加速，并通过混合配置在保持性能的同时显著提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力机制直接从成对分数推导信息流，缺乏显式的关系组织。本文旨在探索一种关系优先的token混合新视角，通过先组织关系再推导信息流的方式，提升模型性能和计算效率。

Method: 提出Relation作为token混合原语，将成对证据组织为显式的Self（自身）和Exchange（交换）关系，再从这些关系中推导信息流。基于此设计了一系列变体：Full Relation（全关系）、FlashRelation（快速关系）、Linear Relation（线性关系）、Hybrid Relation（混合关系）以及KV风格的Relation Cache。

Result: 在约10M、30M、100M三个参数规模的匹配对比中，Full Relation在所有规模下均获得比多头注意力（MHA）更低的最终验证NLL。FlashRelation在固定上下文基准测试中比Full Relation快3.60-4.41倍；在规模匹配的生产负载中，执行Full Relation操作时达到PyTorch FlashAttention 76.4-84.9%的吞吐量。使用75% Linear Relation层的Hybrid Relation实现了良好的语言建模质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持了关系优先的token混合视角：先查询Self，再查询Others，最后让Flow跟随Relation。这种范式为模型设计和优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [120] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向标准化的机器学习电力系统保护评估框架，并在PROTECT-90基准数据集上进行了验证。研究发现分类任务可达到0.991的F1分数，但定位误差为10.20%，且性能对观测条件和决策窗口敏感，为未来可比较、可审计的评估提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在电力系统保护领域的研究常报告近乎完美的性能分数，但这些分数的意义高度依赖于评估设置。保护任务、物理范围、测量方式、时序、目标、预处理和验证方式往往共同变化且未被完整说明，导致评估结果缺乏可比性和可解释性。

Method: 提出标准化导向的评估框架，定义七个必要研究维度：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议和评估输出。在PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准数据集上实例化该框架，包含90 kV双线路拓扑的9022个模拟事件，用于故障分类和定位任务，采用集中式感知、20 ms决策窗口和事件分组验证。

Result: 多层感知机在五折交叉验证中分类任务达到0.991 ± 0.001的宏平均F1分数，定位平均绝对误差为10.20 ± 0.25%线路长度。延长决策窗口至50 ms保持任务相关性能不对称性；降低可观测性使定位误差翻倍但对分类影响较小。传统同步双端定位器在丰富干净信息集下优于学习定位器，测量退化表明干净预测性能不能决定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架将评估假设转化为显式、可复现的证据，为机器学习保护功能的可比性评估、可审计评估及未来面向认证的评估提供了基础，有助于解决当前研究中评估设置不一致导致的性能分数解释困难问题。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [121] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够精确计算局部RLCT的确定性算法，解决了奇异模型（如深度学习）中传统信息准则失效的问题，为模型选择提供了更可靠的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的信息准则（如BIC）依赖于正则性假设，在奇异模型（如深度学习）中会失效，导致错误的模型选择。虽然WBIC通过学习系数解决了这一问题，但学习系数的精确计算仅限于特殊情况，通常只能依赖采样估计方法。

Method: 开发了一个确定性算法，能够针对任意二维模型（其KL距离接触等价于多项式）精确计算局部实对数典范阈值（RLCT），并推导了该算法的复杂度界限。

Result: 该算法在广泛的模型类别上表现有效，包括多项式神经网络。精确计算不仅为校准采样估计器提供了真值基准，还揭示了采样方法无法发现的、学习系数中的代数结构，在浅层情况下速度优于采样方法。

Conclusion: 首次实现了学习系数的精确确定性计算，突破了现有采样方法的局限，为奇异模型的贝叶斯信息准则提供了精确且高效的解决方案，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [122] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用约24万次单词呈现的脑电数据（约49小时），证明了从静默阅读的EEG信号中可以提取开放词汇的单词级信息，解码性能随训练数据量对数线性增长且未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式内隐语音解码面临根本性数据问题：无法收集与大脑活动配对的自发内心独白语料库，而现有的替代范式（提示重复性和回顾性报告生成性内隐语音）采集速度慢、时间锁定差、受试者依从性无法验证。因此，研究者将静默阅读作为可扩展的替代任务，探究对比解码器能从中提取多少词汇和语义信息。

Method: 使用19通道干电极EEG记录单名被试者约24万次单词呈现（393次运行，约49小时）。采用快速序列视觉呈现方式展示连续叙事文本中的单词，每次试验随机化排版以部分去相关单词身份与低级视觉形式。训练卷积EEG编码器（可选后接因果transformer），采用CLIP风格的对比目标，将短EEG窗口与大语言模型中呈现词的隐藏状态嵌入对齐。

Result: 在开放词汇分析中，解码性能（单词分组top-10检索评估）显著高于概率水平，扩展到中频和罕见词，且随训练数据量对数线性增长未见饱和。移除枕叶和后颞叶电极后，单词级增益减少约三分之一，但上下文跟踪保持不变。对照分析将单词级解码与叙事上下文跟踪及transformer位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验分离。

Conclusion: 研究结果证实，静默阅读期间EEG信号中的开放词汇单词级信息是可恢复的，且解码受限于数据量而非已达到饱和状态，为未来研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [123] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DICS框架，通过数据驱动的先验知识构建紧凑的候选分裂点集，显著减少决策树训练时间同时保持预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树模型虽然具有可解释性和强大的实证性能，但训练过程计算开销大，尤其对于大规模高维数据集。主要瓶颈在于每个节点需要对候选分裂点进行穷举搜索，这限制了计算效率。

Method: 提出数据驱动的质心分裂框架(DICS)，这是一种基于聚类的方法，利用数据驱动的先验知识构建紧凑且信息丰富的候选分裂点集合。该方法融合类别感知结构，显著减少分类任务中的分裂搜索空间。DICS可集成到分类树、随机森林和梯度提升模型中。

Result: 理论分析证明，在所述假设下，DICS相比穷举搜索不会降低分类树的性能。大量实验表明，DICS在合成数据集和基准数据集上实现了相当准确率，同时大幅减少训练时间。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的先验知识整合到分裂选择中对可扩展分类树学习具有显著益处，DICS为决策树模型的高效训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [124] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用PCMCI+算法从家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试数据中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚组间因果结构的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动力学复杂且在不同患者群体中存在差异，这阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发。研究旨在通过数据驱动方法揭示不同人群亚组间因果结构的差异。

Method: 使用PCMCI+算法对来自105个HSAT记录的窗口化分数变量进行分析，通过边黑名单机制融入领域知识，并采用bootstrap聚合方法解决亚组样本量小的问题。

Result: 学习到的因果图显示时间自依赖性和呼吸暂停-血氧饱和度下降关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他关系在不同亚组间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果结构在不同性别和年龄亚组间存在系统性差异，这为开发针对性的干预策略提供了重要依据。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [125] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分辨感知的物理支持推断方法，解决了字典学习后稀疏追求在高度相关字典中物理解释不确定的问题，并引入AEB算法避免不合理的精细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度相关的字典中，字典学习后的稀疏追求可能产生精确的原子支持，但其物理解释可能无法被校准数据验证。由于存在多个校准兼容的字典可能对同一支持赋予不同的物理意义，因此需要解决字典不确定性带来的物理推断问题。

Method: 提出了跨字典置信度对应方法，保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，将生存解释投影到物理支持空间。针对局部相关原子类，推导了分辨率的minimax理论界限。计算上引入了主动端点包围（AEB）算法，这是一种自适应有限库过程，仅评估仍能影响物理报告的候选者。

Result: 对于具有分离尺度s的局部相关原子类，当部署数据解析相关块解释时，N个校准信号的最小最大物理分辨率为δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N·s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度Ns⁶决定。部署复制仅在方向变化无法通过调整活跃系数吸收时才能改善物理定位。实验表明点值插入选择器会产生物理过度精度，而AEB避免不支持的精细化且评估次数更少。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够联合考虑学习字典和部署信号表示的不确定性，为物理支持推断提供可靠的置信度评估。AEB算法在有限库实验中表现出色，能够避免不合理的精细化报告，为实际应用提供了更可靠的物理推断框架。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [126] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BERT-LER模型，通过基于百分位数的离散化方法将实验室检测结果编码为标记，结合Integrated Gradients实现可解释性，在7500万患者的EHR数据上预训练和微调，在EHRShot基准测试和哮喘严重程度进展研究中取得与现有模型相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结构化电子健康记录(EHRs)的预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息和相对于输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在实验室值表示与可解释性分离的方法论空白。

Method: 提出BERT-LER模型：(1)采用BERT风格架构；(2)通过基于百分位数的离散化方法将实验室检测结果编码为离散标记，同时保留分级信息；(3)结合Integrated Gradients方法提供基于输入EHR序列的标记级归因；(4)在7500万患者的去识别化EHR数据集上进行预训练和微调；(5)在公开EHRShot基准套件和真实世界哮喘严重程度进展研究上进行评估。

Result: 在EHRShot和哮喘任务中，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开可用基准模型相当，在实验室相关任务上经常超越基准模型；提供的归因与临床已知的风险因素相一致；验证了预测性能和解释性能够泛化到标准临床预测任务之外。

Conclusion: BERT-LER成功统一了实验室值表示和可解释性于单一框架中，填补了EHR基础风格建模的方法论空白；该架构和可解释性方法可应用于使用结构化EHR训练的语言模型，适用于多个治疗领域和预测任务。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [127] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在相同部署条件下对比了吸顶式FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi感知技术，发现IR-UWB在人体活动识别中表现最佳（89.0%宏F1），而FMCW在跨环境泛化方面最优（83.8%宏F1），揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频感知研究中，FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi等技术缺乏在相同部署条件下的公平对比，通常存在硬件、数据集和评估方法差异；同时，具有实际部署和成本优势的吸顶式雷达研究不足，难以指导医疗健康监测系统的技术选型。

Method: 搭建统一的实验平台，对吸顶式FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi感知进行同步数据采集；招募20名参与者，在6种房间布局下采集数据；采用相同的卷积神经网络(CNN)进行评估；设计细粒度10类人体活动识别(HAR)任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务两种实验场景。

Result: IR-UWB在跨被试活动识别任务中取得最高性能（89.0%宏F1）；FMCW在跨未见房间布局的泛化能力最强（83.8%宏F1）；在睡眠监测任务中，所有技术在未见环境中均超过92%宏F1；通过分析距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留等参数，揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的根本权衡机制。

Conclusion: 不同射频技术存在性能与鲁棒性的权衡关系：IR-UWB在识别精度上占优但环境适应性较弱，FMCW在环境泛化上更强但识别精度略低；研究结果为面向医疗健康的射频感知系统设计提供了实用指导，研究者应根据具体应用场景选择合适的技术方案。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [An Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent System for High-Level Bio-Robot Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19699)
*Yujun Chen,Tianle Li,Jiayu Chen,Zhen Yin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了micro_biorobot_agent，一个基于Qwen3.5-27B的离线多智能体系统，用于设计由基因电路编程的生物机器人。该系统整合了23,762条记录的部件库，通过需求分析、检索、组装、冲突检查等流程，在评估中取得了最高分数，并通过来源追踪检查将虚假空白事件减少了80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物机器人的设计需要将应用需求转化为感知、逻辑、输出、组装等模块，同时确保每个选择都有可追溯的部件和证据支持。现有的设计流程缺乏系统化的工具来支持这一复杂的跨学科设计过程。

Method: 开发了一个基于Qwen3.5-27B的离线多智能体系统micro_biorobot_agent，整合了包含23,762条记录的生物部件库。系统流程包括需求分析、模块特定检索、候选组装、冲突检查、局部修复、独立审查和验证。引入了确定性输出检查以确保最终报告与检索的部件集一致，并纠正虚假空白、不支持的部件提及和来源追踪错误。

Result: 在两个作者开发的评估集（各50个查询）上，系统获得了7.35和8.04的平均总分，在七个评估系统中排名最高；在场景设计任务上比第二名高出2.23分。50查询配对消融实验显示，来源追踪检查将虚假空白事件从15次减少到3次（减少80%），并将来源准确性提高了0.75分。

Conclusion: 本文报告了基于Qwen3.5的v1系统，专注于评估高级设计报告而非实验验证的电路。系统展示了多智能体方法在生物机器人设计自动化中的潜力，特别是通过来源追踪机制显著提高了设计的可追溯性和准确性。

Abstract: In this paper, a bio-robot is an engineered living or biohybrid system in which living cells perform one or more core functions, such as sensing, information processing, actuation or output. We focus on systems whose cell-based functions are programmed by genetic circuits; physical movement is optional. Designing such a system requires translating application requirements into sensing, logic or memory, output, assembly, host and containment modules, while grounding each choice in traceable parts and evidence. We present micro_biorobot_agent, an offline multi-agent system built on Qwen3.5-27B. The system combines requirement analysis, module-specific retrieval, candidate assembly, conflict checking, local repair, independent review and validation over an integrated library of 23,762 records covering biological parts, measured combinations, literature-supported relationships and actuation evidence. Deterministic output checks align the final report with the retrieved part set and correct false gaps, unsupported part mentions and source-tracking errors. On two author-developed evaluation sets of 50 queries each, the system obtains mean overall scores of 7.35 and 8.04, the highest among the seven evaluated systems; on Scenario Design it exceeds the runner-up by 2.23 points. A 50-query paired ablation shows that the source-tracking check reduces false-gap incidents from 15 to 3, an 80% reduction, and increases source accuracy by 0.75 points. This paper reports the Qwen3.5-based v1 system and evaluates high-level design reports rather than experimentally validated circuits.

</details>


### [129] [Distilling Aggregate Mobility Statistics into a Language Model Policy for Post-Event Crowd Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19778)
*Tatsuya Amano,Hirozumi Yamaguchi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于语言模型的行人模拟代理微调方法，仅使用聚合统计数据（区域设备计数和OD流量）来训练个体行为模型，无需个人轨迹数据。通过迭代比例拟合和轨迹重采样技术，成功将目的地选择误差降低25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人模拟器需要为每个代理设定行为规则，但隐私限制通常只提供聚合统计数据（区域级设备计数和起点-终点OD流量），缺乏个体轨迹数据。这些聚合数据无法唯一确定个体行为，因为许多不同的决策集合可以产生相同的统计数据。如何仅从聚合数据学习个体行为成为关键挑战。

Method: 1. 使用语言模型作为行人代理的基础架构；2. 从OD流量中读取目标目的地分布；3. 通过迭代比例拟合将模型的目的地分布重新加权至目标分布；4. 针对微调过程中主导目的地类别膨胀的问题，采用低秩适配器对重新采样的轨迹进行训练，采样权重经过校正以补偿膨胀效应；5. 无需推理时校正即可运行微调后的代理。

Result: 在两个棒球比赛的移动网络计数数据上验证，微调后的代理在无需推理时校正的情况下运行，将目的地份额误差降低了25%，同时网格相关性在不同策略间保持相似水平。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了仅使用聚合统计数据训练个体行为模型，通过校正采样策略解决了类别膨胀问题，显著提升了目的地预测精度，为隐私约束下的行人模拟提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Pedestrian simulators need a behaviour rule for every agent, but privacy usually limits the data for setting one to aggregate statistics, namely zone-level device counts and origin-to-destination (OD) flows, with no individual trajectories. Such aggregates under-determine individual behaviour, because many different sets of decisions reproduce the same counts. We fine-tune a language model crowd agent so that the simulated population matches the observed destination composition, the fraction of the departing crowd heading to each point of interest. We read this target from the OD flow and reweight the model's own destination distribution onto it by iterative proportional fitting. Because fine-tuning inflates the dominant destination class, we fit the low-rank adapter to trajectories resampled to a corrected training composition that reaches the target after this inflation. On mobile network counts from two baseball games the fine-tuned agent runs without inference-time correction, cutting the destination-share error by 25%, while the grid correlation remains similar across policies.

</details>


### [130] [Reward-Guided Autoregressive Graph Generation for Efficient Multi-Agent Communication Topology Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20099)
*Poomphob Suwannapichat,Boonyarit Changaival,Caesar Wu,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: RGA-Designer通过引入奖励引导的自回归图生成方法，在保持任务准确率的同时，将LLM多智能体系统的Token消耗平均降低20.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）在复杂推理任务中表现优异，但消耗大量Token。近期工作ARG-Designer将自动拓扑设计重构为自回归图生成问题，但其训练目标缺乏对生成稀疏且高效拓扑结构的显式激励。

Method: 受人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）启发，提出奖励引导的自回归图生成方法（RGA-Designer）。首先训练一个联合捕获任务正确性和结构紧凑性的奖励模型，然后使用该奖励模型作为反馈，对预训练的图生成器进行微调。

Result: 在保持与ARG-Designer相同的任务准确率水平下，Token消耗平均减少20.5%。

Conclusion: 通过引入奖励引导机制，成功解决了自动拓扑设计中缺乏稀疏性激励的问题，实现了在维持性能的同时显著降低计算成本，为LLM多智能体系统的效率优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks by coordinating multiple agents, but at the cost of substantial token consumption. Recent work on automatic topology design, ARG-Designer, has reframed this problem as autoregressive graph generation. However, its training objective provides no explicit incentive for the model to generate sparse and efficient topologies. We address this limitation by introducing a Reward-Guided Autoregressive Graph Generation (RGA-Designer) inspired by Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). We train a reward model that jointly captures task correctness and structural compactness, and then fine-tune the pretrained graph generator using the reward model as feedback. Our method preserves task accuracy at the level of ARG-Designer while reducing token consumption by an average of 20.5%.

</details>


### [131] [Multi-Agent Orchestration with the Common-Sense Reasoning Capabilities of LLMs for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20129)
*Mehdi Azarafza,Faezeh Pasandideh,Ali Ehteshami Bejnordi,Stefan Henkler,Achim Rettberg*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合框架，将LLM常识推理与PPO强化学习和PID控制相结合，在CARLA仿真环境中验证了该方法在多样化自动驾驶场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆需要在多样化且未见过的场景中运行，传统的强化学习和基于规则的方法虽然能提供有效的控制和安全机制，但在需要上下文推理的情况下性能会下降。而LLM虽具备强大的多模态理解与上下文推理能力，直接用于车辆控制却存在延迟和幻觉风险。

Method: 提出了一种混合框架，使用协调器来协调PPO训练的强化学习和PID控制，并在整个框架中应用LLM常识推理。此外，迭代性地利用LLM推理来优化RL奖励函数，以适应动态驾驶环境。在高度随机化的CARLA场景中进行评估，涵盖多样化的环境和交通条件。

Result: 实验结果表明，在保留结构化控制和安全机制的前提下，将基于LLM的推理与传统的自动驾驶方法进行集成具有显著潜力，能够应对高度随机化和多样化的驾驶场景。

Conclusion: 该混合框架成功地将LLM的上下文推理能力融入传统自动驾驶方法中，在保持控制结构和安全性的同时，增强了系统对复杂场景的理解和适应能力，为自动驾驶技术的发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles require robust perception and decision-making capabilities to operate in diverse and unseen scenarios. While reinforcement learning and rule-based methods can provide effective control and safety mechanisms, their performance may degrade in situations requiring contextual reasoning. Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding multimodal information and generating contextual reasoning, however, their use for direct vehicle control can introduce latency and hallucination risks. To address these limitations, a hybrid framework is proposed. This system uses an orchestrator to coordinate PPO-trained reinforcement learning and PID control, with LLM common-sense reasoning applied throughout the framework. LLM reasoning is further employed iteratively to refine the RL reward function for dynamic driving environments. The proposed framework is evaluated in highly randomized CARLA scenarios under diverse environmental and traffic conditions. The results demonstrate the potential of integrating LLM-based reasoning with conventional autonomous driving methods while retaining structured control and safety mechanism.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [HyperCut: Fast Inter-Layer Scheduling via Directed Hypergraph and Early Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19296)
*Ziang Wei,Zirui Xu,Sufeng Guo,Chuanchao Gao,Yiyang Gao,Arvind Easwaran,Yuxiang Fu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HyperCut框架，通过有向超图抽象和早期剪枝策略解决深度神经网络编译器中层间调度的设计空间探索瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的层间调度器需要等待完整的层内细粒度调度完成才能获得成本反馈，这种解耦流程导致反复探索次优或不可行的层间调度，缺乏早期剪枝机制成为DNN编译器设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 基于有向超图(DHG)抽象DNN，提出统一表示State，将DHG划分、瓦片网格分配和张量批次分割联合编码，实现划分与映射的联合优化；通过超图划分实现层间调度的早期过滤，无需求解层内问题即可估算成本上界。

Result: 理论设计空间复杂度从O(9.899^N)降至O(N)；在10个评估案例中，相比SET基线，性能提升2.0倍，探索时间减少80.47%（几何平均）。

Conclusion: HyperCut通过将有向超图划分与映射耦合为统一优化目标，有效解决了层间调度的早期剪枝问题，显著提升了设计空间探索效率和调度性能。

Abstract: As deep neural networks (DNNs) continue to scale, inter-layer scheduling, which orchestrates the spatial allocation of compute resources and the temporal execution order across layers, has become a decisive factor in sustaining high utilization and energy efficiency on tiled accelerators. However, existing inter-layer schedulers defer cost feedback until a complete fine-grained intra-layer scheduling has been resolved. The resulting decoupled flow repeatedly explores sub-optimal or even infeasible inter-layer schedules, and the absence of early pruning during the inter-layer phase remains a critical bottleneck for design-space exploration (DSE) in DNN compilers.
  Our key observation is that the cost of an intra-layer scheduling can be tightly upper-bounded once the inter-layer cut fixes the sub-mesh shape, which lets us cost every inter-layer candidate without solving the intra-layer problem. Hence, we propose a hierarchical partitioning-and-mapping framework, HyperCut, that enables early filtering of inter-layer schedules based on hypergraph partitioning. Based on the directed hypergraph (DHG) abstraction of DNN, we introduce a unified representation, State, that jointly encodes the DHG partition, tile mesh allocation and tensor batch splitting. Thereby, partitioning and mapping are coupled into a union optimization object. For a DNN with N layers, the resulting theoretical design space is bounded by O(N), compared with O(9.899^N) for the state-of-the-art open-source scheduler SET. Across 10 evaluated cases, HyperCut achieves 2.0x performance improvement and 80.47% exploration time reduction over the SET baseline, measured by geometric mean.

</details>


### [133] [Performance Verification of the AmpereOne CPU Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19300)
*Doa'a Al-Otoom,Nicholas Kelly,Aaron Lindsay,Agreen Ahmadi,Ananth Kumar,Anthony Faubert,Benjamin Chaffin,Bret Toll,Eric Wallace,Lucas Crowthers,Mark Charney,Michael Chin,Michael Spradling,Scott Witscher,Sriyash Caculo,Syed Fakhri,Vivek Kothapalli,William Freelove,Mahesh Madhav*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AmpereOne CPU四代产品的工业级性能验证方法，通过RTL与性能模型的周期精确关联、数据驱动的负载筛选及分层验证策略，有效避免了昂贵的后硅bug并确保处理器达成性能目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工艺技术扩展速度放缓，微架构创新成为性能提升的主要动力，这使得硅前性能验证比以往更加关键。传统的功能验证已不足以确保复杂处理器达到性能目标，需要专门的性能验证方法来发现那些功能正确但性能未达预期的问题，以避免昂贵的后硅修复成本。

Method: 该方法论包含三个核心组件：1）RTL设计与跟踪驱动性能模型之间的周期精确关联；2）数据驱动的负载筛选机制，确保验证覆盖关键场景；3）高频日回归系统和统一事件流框架。采用分层策略，首先隔离分支预测单元和L2预取器等单个模块进行精确关联，再推进至全核验证，实现了从局部到整体的系统性验证流程。

Result: 通过分支预测单元和L2预取器的案例研究验证了方法论的有效性。在四代AmpereOne CPU核心的实际应用中，该规范化的迭代过程成功避免了昂贵的后硅bug，确保复杂处理器达到了设计性能目标。分层验证策略有效提升了调试效率，使问题定位更加精准。

Conclusion: 这种严格的、迭代的性能验证方法对于复杂处理器设计至关重要，已成为避免后硅性能bug的不可或缺的手段。随着微处理器行业持续依赖微架构创新来驱动性能提升，性能验证将在未来发挥更加重要的作用。文中最后展望了微处理器行业性能验证的未来发展方向。

Abstract: As process technology scaling slows, microarchitectural innovation has become the primary driver of performance gains, making pre-silicon Performance Verification (PV) more critical than ever. This paper presents the industrial-scale PV methodology applied across four generations of the AmpereOne custom CPU core, centered on the cycle-accurate correlation of the RTL design against a trace-driven performance model. The methodology integrates data-driven workload curation, a high-frequency daily regression system, and a unified event-stream framework for analysis. We demonstrate this methodology through case studies of the Branch Prediction Unit and L2 Prefetcher, highlighting a hierarchical strategy that first isolates individual units for focused correlation before proceeding to full-core verification. The results demonstrate that this disciplined, iterative process is indispensable for avoiding costly post-silicon bugs and ensuring complex processors meet their performance targets. We end with a look towards the future of PV in the microprocessor industry.

</details>


### [134] [HYDRA: A Heterogeneous Chiplet DSE Framework for Serving Dynamic Hybrid LLM Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19395)
*Jiahao Lin,Alish Kanani,Sangwan Lee,Jaehyun Park,Umit Ogras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HYDRA框架，用于异构芯粒系统上混合Transformer-Mamba大语言模型的架构与运行时策略协同设计，实现吞吐量提升1.55倍、TTT降低43.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合Transformer-Mamba大语言模型虽然提升了长上下文效率，但其异构计算与通信模式使得高效硬件加速变得复杂。芯粒架构提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，但跨越静态架构配置和动态运行时策略的设计空间过于庞大，难以穷尽探索。

Method: 提出HYDRA设计空间探索框架，联合探索芯粒组合、布局、芯粒间带宽配置、动态批处理和运行时调度。集成通信感知布局、动态批处理、弹性任务调度，以及基于马尔可夫的快速性能估算器来捕获多租户运行时动态。

Result: 在所有工作负载上，HYDRA平均实现1.55倍的吞吐量提升和43.7%的首令牌时间降低，吞吐量增益最高达2.3倍，相比最先进基线表现显著。

Conclusion: 架构与运行时策略的协同设计对于异构芯粒系统上高效大规模LLM服务至关重要，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Hybrid Transformer-Mamba large language models (LLMs) enhance long-context efficiency, but their heterogeneous computation and communication patterns complicate efficient hardware acceleration. Chiplet-based architectures offer a scalable solution by integrating specialized compute and memory units. However, the design space spanning static architectural configurations and dynamic runtime policies is prohibitively large to explore exhaustively. To address this challenge, we present HYDRA, a comprehensive design space exploration framework for hybrid LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems. HYDRA jointly explores chiplet composition, placement, inter-chiplet bandwidth provisioning, dynamic batching, and runtime scheduling. It integrates communication-aware placement, dynamic batching, elastic task scheduling, and a fast Markov-based performance estimator that captures multi-tenant runtime dynamics for efficient and accurate exploration. Across all workloads, HYDRA delivers 1.55x the throughput and 43.7 percent lower time-to-first-token on average, with throughput gains reaching up to 2.3x compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results highlight that co-designing architecture and runtime policies is critical for efficient large-scale LLM serving on heterogeneous chiplet systems.

</details>


### [135] [Exact Multistate Reliability and Upgrade Design for Heterogeneous HBM Systems via Threshold-Pruned BAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19471)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对HBM系统的部分服务特性建立了多状态可靠性框架，提出了TP-mBAT算法，在保证精确性的同时显著降低了存储开销，并量化了依赖性对可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高带宽内存(HBM)系统由于子通道隔离、通道重映射或保护开销，会呈现部分服务特性：服务单元可能提供完整、降低或零带宽，而非仅有完全服务或完全隔离。传统二元可靠性模型无法准确描述此类系统，需要发展能够处理任意有限带宽状态集合的多状态可靠性评估框架。

Method: 建立多状态可靠性框架，其中每个服务单元携带任意有限带宽状态集合。提出阈值剪枝多状态二叉加法树(TP-mBAT)算法进行精确单阈值评估，并开发精确概率转移敏感性分析方法。针对共享应力场景，引入潜在封装状态混合模型来捕获依赖关系。

Result: 在可复现的14单元非公度基准测试中，TP-mBAT算法与动态规划扩展相同状态空间(6,862节点对6,861次更新)，但保留状态从412,121个概率状态减少到17个遍历条目，峰值存储从25.02 MB降至1,152 B。忽略依赖关系会使可靠性高估8.73个百分点。在紧凑公共网格上，固定网格动态规划仅需273次剪枝DP更新，而TP-mBAT需要362,506次节点访问。

Conclusion: 该框架成功扩展了传统二元k-out-of-n模型，提供了闭环形式的二元映射误差关系来量化平均带宽失真。TP-mBAT算法在非公度场景下实现了显著的存储优化，精确敏感性分析识别了退化状态转移对系统成功的影响，依赖性建模对准确评估HBM系统可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) systems can exhibit partial service rather than only full service or complete isolation: a controller-visible service unit may deliver full, reduced, or zero bandwidth because of sub-channel isolation, lane remapping, or protection overhead. This paper develops an exact multistate reliability framework in which each service unit carries an arbitrary finite set of bandwidth states and reliability is the probability that aggregate delivered bandwidth meets a demand. The binary k-out-of-n model is recovered as a special case, while closed-form binary-mapping error relations quantify mean-bandwidth distortion and provide a screening test for the simpler abstraction. For exact single-threshold evaluation a threshold-pruned multistate Binary-Addition-Tree (TP-mBAT) algorithm is proposed. It is deliberately regime-specific: fixed-grid dynamic programming is preferable on a compact common grid, where a 16-unit commensurate control required 273 pruned-DP updates versus 362,506 TP-mBAT node visits. On a reproducible 14-unit incommensurate benchmark, TP-mBAT is compared against a dynamic program carrying the same threshold rules, so that no baseline is weakened. Both expand the same state space, 6,862 nodes against 6,861 updates, and the separation lies entirely in retained state: 17 traversal entries against 412,121 probability states at central demand, reducing measured peak storage from 25.02 MB to 1,152 B. An exact probability-transfer sensitivity identifies when moving mass from a degraded state to a higher-bandwidth state changes system success, and a reserved-unit floor model admits a third exact pruning rule that is vacuous without such floors. A latent package-state mixture captures shared stress, where ignoring dependence overstates reliability by 8.73 percentage points.

</details>


### [136] [A Thread-Register Decoupled GPU Execution Model for Efficient Tensor Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19628)
*Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yangjie Zhou,Yitong Ding,Guanlin Zhu,Yilu Huang,Chiheng Jin,Chen Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Yu Feng,Anbang Wu,Minyi Guo,Jian Weng,Jiajin Tu,Junsong Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FIBER架构，通过解耦执行实例与寄存器所有权实现动态并行度扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，在现代GPU上为混合精度LLM服务场景实现2.25倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU集成张量核心，但固定并行度和粗粒度调度成为根本瓶颈，特别是现代AI工作负载中GEMM与多样化非GEMM操作交错时，难以高效协调完整的张量计算流水线。

Method: 提出FIBER架构扩展GPU SIMT模型，将基本执行实例fiber与私有寄存器所有权解耦，仅携带最小控制状态并通过共享视图访问SM寄存器；扩展ISA、微架构和编译器实现共享寄存器寻址、无冲突操作数交付和基于fiber的程序映射。

Result: 在典型混合精度LLM服务场景下，FIBER在Ampere上实现2.25倍端到端加速（原始FP16计算为1.15倍），在Hopper和Blackwell上分别实现1.8倍和2.09倍加速，内核级增益高达2.49倍。

Conclusion: FIBER通过动态并行度扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，有效解决了现代GPU张量计算流水线的协调挑战，为矩阵操作数供应提供了无冗余替代方案。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly integrate Tensor Cores into the execution pipeline. Although aggregate tensor throughput continues to grow, aided by an operand supply that has evolved from register-based in Ampere to redundancy-free, memory-based in Hopper and Blackwell, efficiently orchestrating the complete tensor compute pipeline for the modern AI workloads remains challenging. We identify the fundamental bottlenecks as fixed parallelism and coarse-grained scheduling, both of which are exposed by modern AI workloads that interleave diverse non-GEMM operations with GEMM. To orchestrate tensor computation efficiently, we propose FIBER, a new architecture that extends the GPU SIMT (single instruction, multiple thread) model. Its basic execution instance, the \emph{fiber}, is decoupled from private register ownership, carrying only minimal control state while accessing an SM's registers through a shared view. This enables dynamic parallelism scaling, fine-grained register-level dataflow scheduling, and offers a redundancy-free alternative for matrix operand supply. We extend the ISA, microarchitecture, and compiler to realize shared-register addressing, conflict-free operand delivery, and fiber-based program mapping. Under a typical mixed-precision LLM serving scenario, FIBER achieves a 2.25x end-to-end speedup on Ampere (1.15x for the original FP16 computation), with 1.8x and 2.09x on Hopper and Blackwell respectively, and kernel-level gains up to 2.49x.

</details>


### [137] [Automated Estimation of MBIST Area and Test Time in Heterogeneous Memory IPs via Stacked Ensemble Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19705)
*Chee Jin Teoh,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Johnny Kee Hui Wong,Premkumar A/L Kesavan Prabagaran,Muhammad Nadzir Marsono,Nuzhat Khan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种监督学习框架，无需RTL综合即可直接预测MBIST的面积和测试时间开销，面积预测准确率达90.68%，测试时间预测准确率达96.80%（±10%误差范围内），相比基线方法分别提升8.53%和48.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式存储器在AI和边缘计算等高性能SoC设计中占据大量面积，MBIST虽能保证存储器可靠性，但会带来额外的面积和测试时间开销。传统的开销估算方法依赖完整的RTL综合和测试模式生成，过程缓慢且资源消耗大，难以满足设计规划阶段的快速估算需求。

Method: 使用Synopsys Design Compiler和Intel增强的MBIST工具MINT生成数据集（面积4470样本，测试时间624样本），输入特征包括存储器数量、字宽、地址深度、端口配置和时钟域。面积预测采用多项式扩展、对数变换和缩放处理特征，使用XGBoost、LightGBM和神经网络作为基学习器、梯度提升作为元学习器的堆叠集成模型。测试时间预测使用XGBoost和LightGBM结合岭回归，通过100次Optuna搜索优化超参数。

Result: 面积预测准确率为90.68%，测试时间预测准确率为96.80%（均在±10%误差范围内），相比基线方法分别提升8.53%和48.80%。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了MBIST成本的快速估算，支持存储器IP开发中更高效的设计决策，避免了传统RTL综合和测试模式生成的时间与资源开销。

Abstract: Embedded memories occupy a large portion of modern System-on-Chip (SoC) designs, especially in high-performance applications such as artificial intelligence and edge computing. Memory Built-In Self-Test (MBIST) is commonly used to ensure memory reliability, but it introduces additional area and test time overhead. Accurate early estimation of these overheads is important during design planning, yet conventional methods rely on full Register Transfer Level (RTL) synthesis and test pattern generation, which are slow and resource-intensive. This study proposes a supervised learning framework that predicts MBIST area and test time directly from RTL-level design parameters without synthesis. A dataset of 4,470 samples for area and 624 for test time was generated using Synopsys Design Compiler and MINT, an Intel-enhanced MBIST tool. Input features include memory count, word width, address depth, port configuration, and clock domains. For area prediction, the features are processed through polynomial expansion, log transformation, and scaling, followed by a stacked ensemble model using XGBoost, LightGBM, and a Neural Network with Gradient Boosting as the meta-learner. For test time, XGBoost and LightGBM are combined using Ridge Regression, with hyperparameters tuned through a 100-trial Optuna search. The models achieved 90.68% accuracy for area and 96.80% for test time within a +/-10% margin, improving over baseline methods by 8.53% and 48.80% respectively. The results show that this approach enables faster estimation of MBIST costs and supports more efficient design decisions in memory IP development.

</details>


### [138] [Energy-Efficient Visual Inspection with FFT-Based CNNs and Adaptive Floating-Point Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19837)
*Lukas Krupp,Marco Groß,Michael Graichen,Kim Ulrich,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究在工业CPU-FPGA平台上使用FP8量化的FFT卷积神经网络加速，提出两种优化方法，最终实现了84.13%的准确率和2.5倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业CPU-FPGA平台上，研究FFT卷积神经网络如何通过低精度浮点运算（FP8量化）提升推理效率并降低能耗，同时保持模型精度。

Method: 采用FFT卷积与自适应训练后FP8量化相结合，并评估两种面向FPGA的优化方法：FFT内部的渐进式偏置调整（PBA）以及CNN层级的指数偏置选择。在LeNet-5加速器中使用串行基2平方SDF FFT模块实现，并在工业故障检测数据集上验证。

Result: 权重缩放效果优于PBA；层级偏置优化将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%，且无需修改数据路径宽度；相比纯CPU推理，FPGA实现了约2.5倍的能效提升。

Conclusion: FFT卷积结合FP8量化与层级偏置优化可在不改变硬件数据通路的前提下显著提升精度，且FPGA加速在工业场景中具备明显的能效优势。

Abstract: This paper investigates reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT-based CNN inference on an industrial CPU-FPGA platform. We combine FFT-based convolution with adaptive post-training FP8 quantization and evaluate two FPGA-oriented optimization methods: progressive bias adjustment (PBA) within the FFT and layer-wise exponent-bias selection across the CNN. The methods are implemented in a LeNet-5 accelerator using serial radix-$2^2$ SDF FFT modules and evaluated on an industrial fault detection dataset. Results show that weight scaling outperforms PBA, while layer-wise bias optimization increases the accuracy from 80.33% to 84.13% without modifying the datapath width. Compared with CPU-only inference, the FPGA achieves approximately 2.5$\times$ higher energy efficiency.

</details>


### [139] [Experimental Verification of Fast Voltage Droop Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19954)
*Shreyas Srinivas,Ian W. Jones,Carsten Schulze,Milos Krstic,Christoph Lenzen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种全数字自适应电压骤降响应电路，用于解决VLSI电路中因节能减小保护带导致的电压骤降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLSI电路为节能而减小保护带的趋势使电压骤降成为限制工作频率的关键因素，而动态调整时钟频率的现有方法在检测和存储电压骤降时面临亚稳态挑战。

Method: 基于IHP 130 nm工艺实现快速全数字电路，用于自适应响应电压骤降，电路设计的详细描述在配套论文中给出。

Result: 在测试芯片上通过实验验证了该设计的功能正确性和有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的全数字自适应响应电路能够有效应对电压骤降问题，为高性能VLSI电路的节能设计提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Due to the trend towards minimizing guard bands for energy saving purposes, voltage droops are a key limiting factor for the operational frequency of today's VLSI circuits. Adapting clock frequencies dynamically presents the challenge of metastability in the device that detects and stores the existence of voltage droops. We present an implementation of a fast all-digital circuit for adaptive response to droops using the IHP 130 nm process. The description of the design is presented in an accompanying paper. We experimentally validate the functionality of the design on a test chip.

</details>


### [140] [ODEONN: A Digital ODE Solver Architecture for Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20110)
*Bram F. Haverkort,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了首个支持复数值耦合的全数字振荡神经网络通用架构ODEONN，性能损失<2%，能效提升45倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字振荡神经网络架构通常针对特定应用设计，缺乏通用性和可扩展性，需要一种模块化、可扩展且能支持多种ONN应用的通用数字架构。

Method: 提出了ODEONN架构，这是首个支持复数值耦合的全数字ONN架构；引入了一种正弦函数近似方法，相比标准方法节省一半的硬件资源；采用定点数量化方案实现硬件部署。

Result: 与全精度软件仿真相比，性能下降小于2%，证明定点数量化和近似波形对计算精度影响很小；相比传统硬件上的软件仿真，能量-延迟积降低45倍。

Conclusion: ODEONN架构成功实现了通用性、可扩展性和高能效的平衡，为AI和组合优化问题提供了一种高效的数字振荡神经网络解决方案。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) are an alternative computing paradigm for AI and combinatorial optimization problems. However, digital architectures are often designed for specific applications of ONNs. This work introduces a modular and scalable architecture called ODEONN that is generic to multiple applications of ONNs, and to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully digital ONN to also support complex-valued coupling. Additionally, an approximation of the sine function is introduced that uses half of the hardware resources compared to standard methods. The performance of ODEONN is compared with a full-precision software simulation, where a performance degradation of less than $2\%$ is shown. Therefore, we conclude that the fixed-point quantization and the approximated waveform affect the accuracy of computation by only a small amount. Furthermore, ODEONN shows a 45$\times$ reduction in energy-delay product over the software simulation running on conventional hardware.

</details>


### [141] [A Resource-Efficient CNN-Based EEG Auditory Attention Decoding ASIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20198)
*Qier Ma,Richard George,Stefan Scholze,Jehn Constantin,Tobias Reichenbach,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于实时EEG听觉注意力解码的资源高效ASIC，通过量化CNN推理引擎和皮尔逊相关分类器实现，在22nm工艺下仅需0.4941 mW功耗和7.34 ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工耳蜗用户在嘈杂环境中跟踪目标说话者（鸡尾酒会问题）仍面临重大挑战，需要实时EEG听觉注意力解码技术来增强助听设备性能，现有研究缺乏资源高效的硬件实现方案。

Method: 集成量化CNN推理引擎和皮尔逊相关分类器，采用流式执行、片上缓冲和内存高效数据流架构，在GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺下实现，工作电压0.55V。

Result: 芯片面积2.09 mm²（CNN推理引擎和流分类引擎仅占0.076 mm²），功耗0.4941 mW，推理延迟7.34 ms，实现了能源高效的实时EEG解码硬件平台。

Conclusion: 该ASIC设计成功实现了资源高效的实时EEG听觉注意力解码硬件平台，在保持实时性能的同时显著降低了硬件成本，为助听应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Following a target speaker in a noisy environment, commonly known as the cocktail party problem, remains particularly challenging for cochlear implant (CI) users. Recent studies have explored EEG-based auditory attention decoding (AAD) using neural networks to enhance hearing assistance. This paper presents a resource-efficient ASIC for real-time EEG-based auditory attention decoding by integrating a quantized CNN inference engine and a Pearson-correlation classifier. The proposed architecture employs streaming execution, on-chip buffering, and memory-efficient dataflow to reduce hardware cost while maintaining real-time performance.
  The proposed ASIC has been fully implemented in GF22FDX 22-nm CMOS technology, occupying a total silicon area of 2.09 mm$^2$(1264$μ$m x 1654$μ$m), with the CNN inference engine and streaming classification engine requiring only 0.076 mm$^2$. Operating at a core voltage of 0.55 V, the design achieves a power consumption of 0.4941 mW and an inference latency of 7.34 ms, providing an energy-efficient hardware platform for EEG-based auditory attention decoding in hearing-assistance applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Online Scheduling for Throughput Maximization of Time-varying Markovian Channels with Unknown Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19398)
*Tasmeen Zaman Ornee,Clement Kam,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出基于AoCSI的充分统计量解决未知信道统计信息下的无线调度问题，开发Online-MGF算法实现次线性遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于信道统计信息的belief状态方法在统计信息未知时不可行，需要新的方法解决信道状态信息过时的无线调度问题。

Method: 引入信道状态信息年龄(AoCSI)表征CSI陈旧度，证明最新观测CSI及其AoCSI是历史的充分统计量；将问题建模为Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB)，开发在线最大增益优先(Online-MGF)策略。

Result: Online-MGF策略实现关于回合数的次线性遗憾；对ON/OFF信道特例证明了可索引性并推导Whittle指数闭式表达式；数值结果显示算法在少量回合内收敛到已知统计信息的MGF和Whittle指数策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于AoCSI的充分统计量方法有效降低了在线学习的状态空间，Online-MGF算法在未知信道统计信息环境下表现优异，快速收敛到最优策略。

Abstract: We consider a wireless scheduling problem in downlink wireless networks with unknown channel statistics, where a Base Station (BS) sends data to multiple users. The scheduling performance relies heavily on accurate Channel State Information (CSI), which is often costly to acquire. In this paper, CSI is obtained from ACK/NACK feedback, only after each scheduled transmission. Due to limited wireless channel resources, all users cannot be scheduled for transmission simultaneously. Hence, the most recently observed CSI can be outdated.
