The traditional approach to solve scheduling problems using outdated CSI is to utilize belief states, which are calculated using the time correlation statistics of channels. However, channel statistics are often unknown; consequently, belief states can be uncountable and this approach becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce a new sufficient statistics for the wireless scheduling problem. Towards this effort, we characterize the CSI staleness by the Age of Channel State Information (AoCSI) and show that the latest observed CSI and its AoCSI is a sufficient statistic of the history to make the scheduling decisions. Accordingly, we are able to reduce the state space for online learning. Our goal is to develop an online scheduling algorithm that maximizes the expected sum throughput of all users over a finite time-horizon while satisfying a channel resource constraint. The formulated problem is a Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB). We develop an online Maximum Gain First (Online-MGF) policy, which achieves sub-linear regret on the number of episodes. For a special case of ON/OFF channels, we are able to prove indexability and derive a closed-form expression of the Whittle index. Numerical results demonstrate that the Online-MGF policy converges to MGF and Whittle index policies with known statistics within a very few episodes.

</details>


### [143] [Empirical Evaluation of Cross-Carrier MCPTT & OTT MCX Interoperability in High-Density Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19554)
*Eman Hammad,Derek Ladd,Sridhar Kowdley,Walt Magnussen,Michael Fox*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究在德州农工大学Kyle Field球场进行大规模实地测试，发现共享商业基础设施上的MCPTT服务存在关键的网络拥塞瓶颈，优先级QoS可有效缓解，为应急通信网络规划提供了重要参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型群众活动中，跨部门应急响应团队依赖共享商业基础设施部署宽带关键任务即按即说（MCPTT）服务时，面临多运营商环境下的资源竞争问题。本研究旨在评估跨运营商互操作性以及标准QoS与优先级QoS框架在真实网络饱和约束下的性能表现。

Method: 设计了多运营商实证现场实验：在德州农工大学Kyle Field球场（超过105,000名观众）的多个物理区域内，部署12部相同智能手机进行自动化语音通话测试。采用感知客观语音质量分析（POLQA）、数据包传输指标和连接成功率等指标进行监测。

Result: 研究发现语音路径故障源于网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件限制。识别出一种陡峭的非线性网络故障模型：当传输层抖动超过关键阈值时，去抖动缓冲区发生下溢，导致结构性音频降级。优先级管理信道能够有效绕过拥塞问题。

Conclusion: 研究为应急规划人员提供了重要的运营洞察，建议强制要求网络基础设施端到端网络切片和专用资源配置，确保传输层抖动保持在关键故障边界之下，以保障关键任务通信的可靠性。

Abstract: Deploying broadband Mission-Critical Push-To-Talk (MCPTT) services over shared commercial infrastructures introduces resource contention during multi-agency responses in mass-crowd events. This study evaluates cross-carrier interoperability and standard versus prioritized quality of service (QoS) frameworks under real-world saturation constraints. We design an empirical multi-carrier field experiment utilizing twelve identical smartphones deployed across multiple physical sectors inside Texas A&M University's Kyle Field during a football game with 105,000+ attendees. Automated voice calls were monitored using Perceptual Objective Listening Quality Analysis (POLQA), packet delivery metrics, and connection rates. The results reveal that voice path failure is isolated to network infrastructure bottlenecks rather than device hardware limitations. Specifically, we identify a sharp, non-linear network failure model where transport-layer jitter exceeding a critical threshold de-jitter buffer underflows, causing structural audio degradation. Priority-managed channels effectively bypass this congestion. This study helps establish an operational insight for emergency planners to mandate network infrastructure, end-to-end network slicing and dedicated resource provisioning capable of keeping transport-layer jitter below the critical failure boundary.

</details>


### [144] [Vorticity Dissipation Based Routing: A Fluid-Kinetic Framework for Loop-Free Transport in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19630)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流体动力学框架的涡度耗散路由方法（VDR），通过Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观业务流分解为需求驱动的无旋分量和路由涡度分量，实现了路由环路的检测与抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超密集无线网络中离散路由协议受限于信令开销和瞬态路由环路，导致无线资源效率降低。现有的连续体标量密度建模方法缺乏表征这些拓扑异常所需的矢量几何结构，无法有效解决路由环路问题。

Method: 采用流体动力学框架，利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观业务流正交解耦为需求驱动的无旋分量和表示路由涡度的环路诱导螺线管分量。定义网络涡度作为量化拓扑低效性的宏观度量，将路由优化建模为涡度泛函上的梯度流，推导出涡度耗散方程作为控制动态定律。

Result: Lyapunov稳定性分析证明了该机制确保全局涡度单调衰减至渐近无环路平衡态。数值验证表明VDR有效抑制实际转发环路，降低端到端时延，保持稳健的分组投递率，在固定区域密集化下呈现近线性扩展性，并明确考虑了网格相关的泊松求解器开销。

Conclusion: VDR框架成功将流体动力学理论应用于网络路由问题，为超密集无线网络提供了一种可扩展且理论完备的环路抑制解决方案，在保证网络性能的同时实现了复杂度的可控性。

Abstract: Discrete routing protocols in ultra-dense wireless networks are constrained by signaling overhead and transient routing loops that degrade radio-resource efficiency. While continuum modeling provides a scalable alternative, existing scalar density approaches lack the vector geometric structure required to characterize these topological anomalies. This paper introduces a fluid-kinetic framework, vorticity dissipation-based routing (VDR), utilizing the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition. We demonstrate that the macroscopic traffic flux can be orthogonally decoupled into a demand-driven irrotational component and a loop-induced solenoidal component representing routing vorticity. Building on this insight, we define network vorticity as a macroscopic metric to quantify topological inefficiency. Routing optimization is formulated as a gradient flow on an enstrophy functional, yielding a vorticity dissipation equation as the governing dynamic law. Lyapunov stability analysis proves that this mechanism ensures the monotonic decay of global enstrophy toward an asymptotically loop-free equilibrium. Numerical results validate that VDR suppresses realized forwarding loops, reduces end-to-end delay, maintains robust packet delivery, and exhibits near-linear scaling under fixed-area densification while explicitly accounting for the grid-dependent Poisson-solver cost.

</details>


### [145] [Digital Tides: A Fluid-Dynamic Framework for Flux-Aware Infrastructure Provisioning in UAV Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19638)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对高频脉冲式物流无人机群形成的“数字浪潮”现象，提出了基于流体力学模型的时空资源调度框架，通过求解连续性方程获取工作负载流的宏观速度场，设计了通量感知的非对称激活策略，实现了对移动需求的零延迟追踪，在服务可靠性和能耗之间达到了帕累托最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基础设施资源供给策略依赖于静态快照分析和局部密度估计，无法捕捉计算工作负载的宏观对流特性，导致反应式资源激活存在固有滞后性，在推进波前处以牺牲任务关键型服务为代价换取名义效率增益，无法有效应对物流无人机群带来的复杂交通动态。

Method: 建立基于流体的时空框架，通过显式求解连续性方程刻画工作负载流的宏观速度场；提出通量感知的非对称激活策略，利用推导的信息通量向量作为需求传播的运动学先兆，解耦激活和去活动态；推导瞬时服务可用性和周期平均能效的闭式表达式，并构建考虑服务质量惩罚的有效能效度量指标。

Result: 理论分析证实了通量信号的固有空间相位超前特性，所提策略生成了主动保护环以补偿服务建立延迟（包括移动边缘计算容器冷启动延迟）；数值结果表明，通量驱动策略实现了对移动波前的零延迟追踪，在动态物流走廊中在服务可靠性与能耗间达到帕累托最优权衡，性能优于反应式基准方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于流体力学模型的通量感知非对称激活策略有效解决了高频脉冲式物流无人机群场景下的资源供给滞后问题，通过主动预测性资源调度补偿服务建立延迟，为零延迟移动波前追踪提供了理论保证和实用方案，显著优于传统反应式方法。

Abstract: The emergence of high-frequency pulsating logistics unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms gives rise to ``Digital Tides'', i.e., complex traffic dynamics that challenge sustainable resource provisioning in mobile computing networks. Conventional infrastructure provisioning strategies, which typically rely on static snapshot-based analysis and localized density estimation, fail to capture the macroscopic advection of computational workloads. As a result, reactive resource activation suffers from inherent hysteresis, yielding nominal efficiency gains at the cost of mission-critical service loss at the advancing wavefront. To address this issue, we develop a fluid-based spatiotemporal framework by explicitly solving the continuity equation to characterize the macroscopic velocity field of the workload flow. Building on this framework, we propose a flux-aware asymmetric activation strategy that leverages the derived information flux vector as a kinematic precursor of demand propagation. Unlike symmetric thresholding, the proposed control logic decouples activation and deactivation dynamics. Theoretical analysis confirms the intrinsic spatial phase-lead of the flux signal and shows that the proposed strategy generates a proactive guard ring to compensate for service setup latency, including delays caused by mobile edge computing container cold-starts. We further derive closed-form expressions for instantaneous service availability and period-average energy efficiency. In addition, we formulate a quality-of-service-penalized metric to evaluate effective energy efficiency under strict outage constraints. Numerical results show that the proposed flux-driven strategy enables zero-latency tracking of the mobile wavefront and achieves a Pareto-optimal trade-off between service reliability and energy consumption, outperforming reactive baselines in dynamic logistics corridors.

</details>


### [146] [RFWM: Physics-Guided World Model for Dynamic Wireless Radiance Field Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19709)
*Zijiu Yang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RFWM，一种物理引导的射频世界模型，通过两阶段训练策略和物理约束，实现了对动态和未见环境中的射频辐射场建模，在MSE指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频辐射场建模对于无线网络优化和感知至关重要，但现有基于学习的方法在动态和未见环境中泛化能力不足，难以准确合成射频场。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段将预训练的视觉扩散骨干网络适应到射频轨迹预测，并引入Friis引导先验进行粗衰减指导；第二阶段从头训练ControlNet并微调骨干网络，通过六种物理引导正则化器强制细粒度传播一致性，跨高度头同时生成查询接收器高度的射频轨迹。

Result: 构建了包含7,715个序列、平均33帧、覆盖115个环境的动态射频场生成基准。实验结果表明，RFWM在分布内和分布外设置下，MSE分别比现有最优方法降低约7 dB和3 dB。

Conclusion: RFWM通过物理引导的先验和约束，有效提升了射频辐射场建模在动态和未见环境中的泛化能力和准确性，为无线网络优化和感知提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) radiance-field modeling is essential for wireless network optimization and sensing, yet remains challenging in dynamic and unseen environments. Existing learning-based methods synthesize RF fields from sparse measurements, but most struggle to generalize to dynamic and unseen environments. To address this limitation, we propose RFWM, a physics-guided RF world model that maps multimodal physical conditions like visual dynamics and AP configurations to spatiotemporal RF fields. RFWM adopts a two-stage training strategy with physics-guided priors and constraints. In the first stage, RFWM adapts a pretrained visual diffusion backbone to RF trajectories to predict RF sequences from a few past RF inputs, while conditioning the backbone on a Friis-guided prior for coarse attenuation guidance. In the second stage, RFWM learns the physical-to-RF mapping by training a ControlNet from scratch and fine-tuning the RF-adapted backbone, while six physics-guided regularizers enforce fine-grained propagation consistency. Cross-height heads then jointly generate RF trajectories at queried receiver heights in one forward pass. We construct a new benchmark of 7,715 sequences averaging 33 frames across 115 environments for dynamic RF-field generation. Experimental results show that RFWM improves MSE by approximately 7 dB and 3 dB over the state of the art under in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, respectively.

</details>


### [147] [Proper Sea Surface Roughness Enhances the Performance of Near-Shore Maritime Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19730)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Shaoshi Yang,Song Zhao,Jinyang Yu,Weiliang Xie,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于随机几何的近岸海上无线通信物理分析框架，通过引入粗糙度感知的反射系数，首次实现了动态海况下的覆盖概率和平均速率的解析表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近岸海上无线通信分析模型通常采用过于简化的传播假设（如理想光滑海面），无法准确捕捉海上信道的完整动态特性，导致性能分析不够准确。需要建立一个能够反映真实海面粗糙度影响的分析框架。

Method: 1. 使用非齐次泊松点过程对船舶空间分布进行建模，反映真实的近港口密度分布；2. 基于经典瑞利准则推导出新型反射系数，将路径损耗与有效波高显式关联，替代理想光滑海面假设；3. 将粗糙度感知信道模型整合到随机几何框架中，推导上行链路覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表达式。

Result: 1. 揭示了依赖海况的可靠性-容量权衡：粗糙度引起的相干镜面反射衰减可以抑制破坏性干扰零点并提高面向可靠性的覆盖，但会降低高信噪比性能和平均速率；2. 现有测量数据支持粗糙度敏感反射机制，但在粗糙海况下的直接VHF验证仍然缺乏；3. 跨频率消融研究确认了粗糙度效应的波长依赖性，表明反射系数必须根据工作频率进行评估。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了首个能够处理动态粗糙海面条件下聚合干扰的可解析随机几何框架，为近岸海上无线通信系统的可靠性和容量优化提供了理论依据。该框架揭示了海况对通信性能的关键影响，为实际海上通信系统设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Accurate performance analysis for near-shore maritime wireless communication is essential for ensuring robust and reliable operations. However, existing analytical models often rely on oversimplified propagation assumptions, such as a perfectly smooth sea surface, which fail to capture the full dynamics of the maritime channel. In this paper, we develop a physically grounded analytical framework using stochastic geometry that bridges this gap. The spatial distribution of vessels is modeled as a non-homogeneous Poisson point process to reflect realistic near-port densities. We replace the idealized smooth-sea assumption by deriving a novel reflection coefficient from the classical Rayleigh criterion, which explicitly links the path loss to the significant wave height. Integrating this roughness-aware channel model into the stochastic geometry framework, we derive new analytical expressions for the uplink coverage probability and average ergodic rate, providing the first tractable characterization of aggregate interference under such dynamic conditions. The analysis reveals a sea-state-dependent reliability--capacity trade-off: roughness-induced attenuation of the coherent specular reflection can suppress destructive-interference nulls and improve reliability-oriented coverage, while reducing high-SINR and average-rate performance. Available measurements support the underlying roughness-sensitive reflection mechanism, but direct VHF validation under rough sea conditions remains unavailable; the corresponding rough-sea results are therefore interpreted as model-based predictions. A cross-frequency ablation further confirms the wavelength dependence of the roughness effect and shows that the reflection coefficient must be evaluated for the operating frequency.

</details>


### [148] [Using Zone-Disjoint Multi-Path Routing Algorithm for Video Transmission over Ah-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20148)
*Ali Asghar Khavasi,Nastooh Taheri Javan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对自组织网络中多路径路由问题，提出了一种基于区域不相交路径的新型路由算法，并结合视频传输方案，实现了比传统节点不相交路径算法更优的视频传输性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自组织网络中，尽管网格拓扑结构看似便于发现多路径，但由于CSMA/CA机制的影响，传统方法找到的路径并非完全不相交。节点不相交路径在实际网络中仍会因相邻节点的静默要求导致端到端延迟增加和误码率上升，无法保证路径间的独立性，因此需要设计考虑网络固有特性的新型多路径路由算法。

Method: 提出了一种新型的多路径路由算法，该算法选择区域不相交路径（zone-disjoint paths）而非传统的节点不相交路径。区域不相交路径考虑了自组织网络的介质访问机制和邻域通信模式，避免了中间节点因避让邻居而产生的静默问题。同时，该算法结合了适合视频传输的方案，在发现的路径上进行多路径视频传输。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比于以往使用的算法，本文提出的新算法配合适当的视频传输方案，在自组织网络上获得了更优的视频传输性能，有效改善了端到端延迟和误码率问题。

Conclusion: 在自组织网络的多路径视频传输中，采用区域不相交路径的路由算法能够有效克服传统节点不相交路径的局限性，提升网络性能。这证明了在设计多路径路由算法时，必须考虑网络的介质访问机制和邻域通信模式等固有特性。

Abstract: Finding multi-path routes in ad hoc networks due to their grid topology seems to be a trivial task, but because of CSMA/CA effects in these networks found paths are not completely disjoint unless an appropriate algorithm have taken into account. If such an algorithm provided and designed carefully it could improve multi-path video transmission over these kinds of networks. By using node-disjoint paths, it is expected that the end-to-end delay and BER in each case should be independent of each other. However, because of natural properties and medium access mechanisms in ad hoc networks the end-to-end delay and also BER between any source and destination depends on the pattern of communication in the neighborhood region. In this case some of the intermediate nodes should be silent to reverence their neighbors and this matter increases the end-to-end delay. To avoid this problem, multi-path routing algorithms can use zone-disjoint paths instead of node-disjoint paths. In this paper we demonstrated a new multi-path routing algorithm that selects zone-disjoint paths that in addition used for video multi-path transmission over ad hoc networks. It is shown that by using this new algorithm along with choosing an appropriate scheme for video transmission over discovered paths, our approach receives enhanced results comparing previously used algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [The Lazy Pod That Lies: Deferred Cost and Failure Semantics of Lazy Container Image Pulling for Model Serving on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19412)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究评估了Kubernetes环境下模型服务中容器镜像懒加载技术。虽然显著降低了冷启动时间（从24.5-573秒降至16.9-17.6秒），但发现了严重的缓存耗尽故障模式：在持续读取下，节点级缓存耗尽会导致运行中Pod读取模型文件失败，且故障在系统监控可见前196秒就已发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器镜像懒加载技术承诺通过立即挂载镜像并按需获取内容来消除模型服务Pod启动的主要成本。研究者希望评估这一承诺在Kubernetes模型交付场景中的实际效果，包括性能收益和潜在风险。

Method: 在Kubernetes环境使用KServe框架，对比评估两种生产级懒加载系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI）与急切加载基线的性能。测试覆盖2GB到140GB的模型工件（包括真实fp16权重），测量冷启动时间、读取延迟、缓存行为和故障模式。

Result: 懒加载确实实现了冷启动时间与镜像大小无关（16.9-17.6秒 vs 急切加载24.5-573.0秒），但代价被延迟而非消除：14GB模型完整读取需105.3秒，慢于急切加载的72.4秒。两个系统在生命周期两端支付代价：SOCI在Ready前预取几乎完整镜像，eStargz将几乎所有内容延迟到首次读取。更关键的是，发现了急切加载结构性上不可能出现的故障模式：在持续合法读取下，快照器的节点级缓存耗尽有限卷空间，导致已运行Pod开始读取模型文件失败。在缓存耗尽最早阶段，Pod在196秒内通过了所有Kubernetes可见和应用级检查，但其快照器已记录真实故障；在更大压力下，67-94%模型文件失败，失败率随缓存占用率单调增长。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了懒加载技术的隐性成本和严重故障风险，为服务平台的镜像放置、监控和缓存配置提供了指导建议。需要谨慎设计缓存大小、监控机制和故障恢复策略，以确保生产环境中的可靠性。

Abstract: Lazy container-image pulling promises to eliminate the dominant cost of starting a model-serving pod by mounting the image immediately and fetching content on demand. We evaluate this promise for model delivery on Kubernetes, using KServe with two production lazy-pulling systems -- eStargz/stargz-snapshotter and AWS SOCI -- against eager baselines, on artifacts from 2 to 140 GB including real fp16 weights. Lazy pulling delivers its headline: cold time-to-first-prediction becomes size-independent (16.9--17.6s, versus 24.5--573.0s eager). But the cost is deferred, not eliminated: a full read of a 14 GB model through the lazy mount takes 105.3s, slower than the 72.4s eager pull it replaced, and the two systems pay at opposite lifecycle ends (SOCI prefetches nearly the full image before Ready; eStargz defers nearly everything to first read). More consequentially, we characterize a failure mode eager pulling structurally cannot exhibit: under sustained legitimate reads with default configuration, the snapshotter's node-level cache exhausts its finite volume and already-running pods begin failing reads of model files. At the earliest stage of exhaustion, an instrumented serving pod passed every Kubernetes-visible and application-level check for 196s while its snapshotter was already logging real failures; under heavier pressure, 67--94% of model files fail, scaling monotonically with residual cache occupancy. A live pod self-heals if cache space is freed under it, but a snapshotter-daemon restart under a live pod leaves permanently stale file handles in a pod still reported Running. We derive placement, monitoring, and cache-sizing guidance for serving platforms and operators.

</details>


### [150] [When Do LLM Agents Help? Deadline-Aware Mixed-Criticality Task Scheduling at the Autonomous-Vehicle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19557)
*Reza Zakerian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究异构MEC服务器上的截止期感知混合关键性任务调度。作者首先提出了一种基于拍卖的强启发式算法，在平稳负载下接近最优；然后引入多智能体LLM控制层，发现在非平稳负载（如安全关键任务激增）时LLM才能显著优于静态启发式算法和Bandit方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆将延迟敏感的感知任务卸载到MEC服务器，错失安全关键任务会导致危险而非性能降级。LLM被提议作为自适应可解释调度器，但缺乏关于何时有效的实证证据。研究旨在探索多智能体LLM控制层是否能在强启发式基础上带来改进。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1)构建窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法，按最早截止期优先排序时间关键任务，按最早完成时间放置任务；(2)添加LLM控制平面进行对比实验。在3种拓扑、60个实例、15个基线上进行评估，使用受控分解分析各组件贡献。

Result: 启发式算法TC完成率达0.902，超越所有基线（最佳基线0.838，p<0.001），达到CP-SAT上界的87%。平稳负载下LLM无增益；任务激增时LLM显著优于静态启发式和Bandit。LLM仅在非平稳性创造了固定策略无法利用的优化空间时才体现价值。

Conclusion: LLM编排机制仅在非平稳负载条件下才能证明其成本合理性，此时固定策略无法有效应对动态变化。平稳负载下强启发式已接近最优，LLM控制层无附加价值。作者公开了代码和种子实例以支持复现。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offload latency-sensitive perception tasks to nearby mobile edge computing (MEC) servers, where a missed safety-critical task is unsafe rather than merely degraded. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as adaptive, explainable schedulers, yet evidence of when they help is scarce. We study deadline-aware, mixed-criticality scheduling on heterogeneous MEC servers, where time-critical (TC) tasks must be protected at a controlled cost to best-effort traffic, and ask whether a multi-agent LLM control layer improves on a strong heuristic. We answer in two steps. First we build the heuristic: a windowed contract-net auction that orders each admission window time-critical-first by earliest deadline and places tasks by earliest-finish-time. Across 60 instances on three topologies and 15 baselines under an identical online constraint, it attains a TC completion rate of 0.902, above every baseline (Holm-corrected p < 0.001; best baseline 0.838) and at 0.87 of a CP-SAT upper bound. Second, we add the LLM control plane. A controlled decomposition traces the scheduler's advantage to two ordinary factors, the batching horizon and time-critical-first ordering; the auction, the per-window LLM policy, and online adaptation add nothing while the load is stationary, where the heuristic is already near-optimal. Under a mid-run surge of safety-critical tasks the picture changes, and the LLM control plane gains significantly over both the static heuristic and the bandit. LLM orchestration therefore earns its cost only when non-stationarity opens headroom a fixed policy cannot use. We report control-plane latency and rationale, and release all code and seeded instances.

</details>


### [151] [Optimality and Trade-offs in Fast BFT SMR (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19629)
*Neil Giridharan,Minh Tung Nguyen,Ittai Abraham,Allen Clement,Natacha Crooks,Pierre Sutra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了拜占庭容错快速状态机复制（Fast BFT SMR）复制因子的紧确上下界，并提出了一个展示复制因子与恢复效率权衡的次优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速状态机复制协议在崩溃故障模型下已得到广泛研究，具有低延迟、高吞吐、高可用性和公平性等优势；然而在拜占庭容错场景下研究甚少，现有Fast BFT SMR协议数量有限，因此亟需对其理论基础进行深入分析。

Method: 理论证明方法：给出Fast BFT SMR复制因子的上界和下界证明；协议设计：提出一个次优协议，通过具体构造展示复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡关系。

Result: 确定了Fast BFT SMR协议所需复制因子的紧确上下界（tight upper and lower bounds）；发现复制因子与恢复效率之间存在内在的权衡关系，并通过具体协议实例验证了这一发现。

Conclusion: 本文填补了拜占庭容错快速状态机复制理论基础的空白，为未来设计更高效的Fast BFT SMR协议提供了理论指导和设计空间边界。

Abstract: Fast state-machine replication (SMR) protocols in the crash-fault setting have attracted significant interest in both academia and industry. This interest stems from their advantages over leader-based protocols, including low execution latency for non-conflicting commands, high throughput, improved availability, and increased fairness. While Fast SMR is well studied in the crash-fault setting, the Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) setting remains much less understood, with only a small number of existing Fast BFT SMR protocols. In this paper, we present tight upper and lower bounds on the replication factor required for Fast BFT SMR. We also present a suboptimal protocol that illustrates a trade-off between replication factor and recovery efficiency.

</details>


### [152] [CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19677)
*Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheRoute通过周期性路由规划平衡了缓存亲和性与负载均衡，在Llama-3.3-70B上实现2.3倍吞吐提升，KV缓存命中率从64.1%提升至93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存仅在请求返回到仍持有前缀KV的服务器时才能避免预填充。缓存无感知的负载均衡会分散这种复用，而固定亲和性虽能保留复用却可能导致服务器过载。需要一种方法来权衡这一矛盾。

Method: 提出CacheRoute，采用周期性路由规划策略。将高频请求键接入稳定的预热集合，并根据预期负载进行放置。热键可使用多个目的地，通过半合成聚合和受控实验验证方法有效性。

Result: 在60个H100 GPU上运行Llama-3.3-70B (fp8)时，CacheRoute在p99延迟3.5秒的SLO下维持176±11 QPS，是五个基线中最强者的2.3倍。服务KV缓存命中率从缓存无感知均衡下的64.1±1.3%提升至93.2±0.5%。

Conclusion: 亲和性并非总能带来改进，当亲和性恢复的KV工作量过少时，残余负载偏斜会减少或消除改进效果。建议在部署前使用影子回放进行验证，而非仅依赖工作负载统计就启用亲和性。

Abstract: Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3-70B in fp8 across 60 H100 GPUs, CacheRoute sustains 176+/-11 QPS at a 3.5-s p99 SLO, 2.3x the strongest of five baselines. Served KV-cache hit rate rises from 64.1+/-1.3% under cache-blind balancing to 93.2+/-0.5%. A second semi-synthetic aggregate and controlled 8B and burst experiments separate the effects of affinity and placement. Two 32B workloads provide the counterexamples: when affinity recovers too little KV work, its residual load skew reduces or erases the improvement. We therefore recommend gating any deployment with a shadow replay rather than enabling affinity from workload statistics alone.

</details>


### [153] [ParaWeb: Parallel Programming Patterns for Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19935)
*Suejb Memeti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ParaWeb，一个TypeScript并行编程库，在Web环境下实现了10种并行编程模式，提供消息传递、共享缓冲区和GPU三种实现变体。实验显示CPU变体最高加速11.6倍，GPU变体最高加速260倍，图像卷积案例中GPU加速达414倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用需要计算密集型处理，但JavaScript传统上限于单线程执行模型。虽然Node.js Worker Threads和浏览器Web Workers提供了底层并行执行机制，但开发者缺乏捕获可重用模式的高级抽象。

Method: 实现了ParaWeb TypeScript库，包含10种并行编程模式，支持服务端Node.js、客户端浏览器和WebGPU计算着色器。每种模式提供三种实现变体：基于postMessage的消息传递(MP)变体、使用SharedArrayBuffer的共享缓冲区(Shared)变体、以及使用WebGPU计算着色器的GPU变体。在三种数据规模下评估了全部30个实现的性能。

Result: CPU变体在16线程下对计算密集型模式实现高达11.6倍加速；GPU变体对Farm、Scatter、Reduce和Map等高算术强度模式达到260倍加速。图像卷积滤波器案例研究显示，GPU加速在不可分离核上达到414倍加速，并在1024×1024、2048×2048和4K图像上保持一致的性能扩展。

Conclusion: ParaWeb成功填补了Web并行编程高级抽象的空白，通过提供多种实现变体和GPU加速，显著提升了Web应用的计算性能，证明了Web环境并行计算的可行性和高效性。

Abstract: Modern web applications increasingly require computationally intensive processing, yet JavaScript, the dominant language of the web, has traditionally been limited to a single-threaded execution model. Node.js Worker Threads and browser Web Workers provide low-level mechanisms for parallel execution, but developers lack high-level abstractions that capture recurring parallel structures as reusable patterns. In this paper, we present ParaWeb, a TypeScript library that implements ten parallel programming patterns for server-side Node.js, client-side browser environments, and WebGPU compute shaders. ParaWeb provides three implementation variants for each pattern: a message-passing (MP) variant based on structured cloning via postMessage, a shared-buffer (Shared) variant that uses SharedArrayBuffer with typed array views, and a GPU variant that uses WebGPU compute shaders for hardware-accelerated execution. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and pattern-specific implementation strategies, and we evaluate the performance of all thirty implementations across three data sizes. Experimental evaluation results show that the CPU-based variants achieve speedups of up to 11.6x with 16 threads for compute-bound patterns, while the GPU variants reach speedups of up to 260x for compute-bound patterns with high arithmetic intensity such as Farm, Scatter, Reduce, and Map. A case study on five image-convolution filters further shows that GPU acceleration reaches up to 414x speedup over single-threaded CPU on non-separable kernels, with consistent scaling across 1024x1024$, 2048x2048$, and 4K images.

</details>


### [154] [Design and Empirical Evaluation of a Network-Centric, On-Premises Architecture for Earth Observation Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20283)
*João Pinelo,João Gonçalves,Denis Willett,Amit Ruhela,Derek Steinmoeller,Uriel Mendoza,Pelumi S. Alao,Ronald Soares Lopes,Rogerio Atem de Carvalho,Pedro Mattos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复制的本地化地球观测数据访问架构，通过网络带宽隔离测试发现：网络带宽是数据吞吐量的主要瓶颈，但在超过10 Gbps/服务器的阈值后，端点内存拓扑结构成为新的决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测计划产生的数据量超过大多数机构网络的传输和存储能力，而跨大西洋流域的机构面临连接性、主权和资金限制，使得本地部署基础设施成为唯一可行路径。云原生数据格式的性能依赖于底层网络带宽，但这一依赖关系很少被单独测量和评估。

Method: 在大西洋云创始节点AIR数据中心构建测试系统，包含MinIO对象存储集群（100 GbE网络）、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层。通过持续并行负载测试网络结构，评估对象存储吞吐量，并与相同硬件上的带宽限制基准进行对比，从而将网络带宽作为唯一变量进行隔离测试。同时与合作伙伴机构进行多站点复制基准测试。

Result: 网络带宽是批量EO数据访问存储吞吐量的主导约束，直到达到特定阈值（本硬件类别中高于10 Gbps/服务器）。低于该阈值时，网络容量单独决定设施交付能力；高于该阈值时，端点内存拓扑结构（而非容量）决定系统能够利用的带宽量，进一步的网路投资回报取决于端点内存配置。

Conclusion: 为资源受限机构提供了可复制的本地化EO数据访问架构方案。研究表明网络投资策略应分阶段进行：网络带宽是首要瓶颈，但在超过阈值后，端点内存配置可以延后购买，这为机构提供了灵活的基础设施规划依据。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) programmes generate data at volumes that exceed the transfer and storage capacity of most institutional networks. Public cloud platforms address this for well-resourced organisations, but institutions across the Atlantic basin face constraints in connectivity, sovereignty and funding that make on-premises infrastructure the only viable path. Cloud-native data formats enable efficient partial reads, yet their performance depends on the bandwidth of the underlying network fabric, a dependency rarely measured in isolation. This paper presents a replicable, network-centric architecture for on-premises EO data access, evaluated at its first operational deployment: the AIR Data Centre, founding node of the Atlantic Cloud. The system comprises a MinIO object storage cluster on a 100 GbE fabric, a PostGIS metadata catalogue and an OGC API-EDR access layer. We characterise the fabric under sustained parallel load, evaluate object storage throughput for EO-representative workloads, and compare measured performance against throttled baselines on identical hardware, isolating network bandwidth as the sole variable. Multi-site replication benchmarks with partner institutions characterise the federation primitive the model depends on. Network bandwidth is the dominant constraint on storage throughput for bulk EO data access up to a threshold; beyond it, endpoint memory topology rather than capacity governs how much bandwidth a system can use. For this hardware class that threshold lies above 10 Gbps per server. Below it, network capacity alone sets what the facility can deliver; above it, the return on further network investment depends on endpoint memory provisioning, which can be deferred and bought later.

</details>
